###############################################################################
# WHG_chaos_effects.txt: scripted effects
# Subject: Chaos: the four gods and Undivided, cults, daemons, the Doom,
# mutation, beastmen, skaven, Norsca
#
# A scripted effect is a named list of commands, used anywhere an effect can go
# as "name = yes". It runs in the scope it is called from. Names are global
# across the folder and must be unique; the load order of files does not
# matter.
#
# Each entry lists the files that use it ("Used by"). Sections:
#   Shared: used by three or more files (23)
#   Used by WHG_beastmen_events (14)
#   Used by WHG_black_crusade_events (6)
#   Used by WHG_carnival_events (15)
#   Used by WHG_chaos_cult_events (22)
#   Building blocks of other entries in this file (15)
#   Used by WHG_chaos_gods_events (17)
#   Used by WHG_chaos_horde_events (11)
#   Used by WHG_chaos_undivided_events (45)
#   Used by WHG_daemon_events (11)
#   Used by WHG_doom_events (5)
#   Used by WHG_slaanesh_events (10)
#   Used by WHG_warpstone_events (6)
#   Other helpers used by one or two files (10)
#   Not used by any live script yet: kept for content to come (2)
###############################################################################

###############################################################################
# Shared: used by three or more files
###############################################################################

# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events, WHG_chaos_effects, WHG_chaos_gods_events
give_has_bubonic_plague_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { trait = has_bubonic_plague } }

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Reapers"
				NOT = { has_character_flag = developing_illness }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = disease_not_serious }
			}
			set_character_flag = got_bubonic_plague
			set_character_flag = developing_illness
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = symptom.13 days = 3 random = 10 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } }
			add_trait = has_bubonic_plague hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = has_bubonic_plague } }
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_character_effects, WHG_chaos_cult_events, WHG_chaos_effects
give_syphilitic_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { trait = syphilitic }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Reapers"
				NOT = { has_character_flag = developing_illness }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = disease_not_serious }
			}
			set_character_flag = got_syphilis
			set_character_flag = developing_illness
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = symptom.15 days = 3 random = 40 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } }
			add_trait = syphilitic hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = syphilitic } }
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events, WHG_chaos_effects +4 more
give_food_poisoning_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { trait = food_poisoning }
			has_dlc = "Reapers"
			NOT = { has_character_flag = developing_illness }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = disease_not_serious }
		}

		set_character_flag = got_food_poisoning
		set_character_flag = developing_illness
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = symptom.24 days = 1 random = 2 } }
	}
	else_if = { # without Reaper's Due: plain illness
		limit = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } }
		give_ill_effect = yes
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events, WHG_chaos_effects, WHG_assassins_events
give_cancer_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { trait = cancer }
			has_dlc = "Reapers"
			NOT = { has_character_flag = developing_illness }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = disease_not_serious }
		}

		set_character_flag = got_cancer
		set_character_flag = developing_illness
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = symptom.26 days = 30 random = 60 } }
	}
	else_if = { # without Reaper's Due: plain illness
		limit = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } }
		give_ill_effect = yes
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects, WHG_personality_events +5 more
give_ill_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_dlc = "Reapers"
				trait = ill
			}
		}
		add_trait = ill hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = ill } }
		hidden_tooltip = {
			character_event = {
				days = 3
				id = traitnote.290 #Notify Ill
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dlc = "Reapers" }
		tooltip = { add_trait = flu hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = flu } } }
		give_flu_effect = yes
	}
}

# Impregnation and venereal disease handling between event_target:sex_partner
# and THIS. Used in THIS scope.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events, WHG_armoury_events +2 more
unprotected_sex_effect_event_target = {
	random = {
		chance = 50
		venereal_disease_effect_event_target = yes
	}
	random = {
		chance = 10
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			is_fertile_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			event_target:sex_partner = { is_fertile_trigger = yes }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			is_infertile_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			event_target:sex_partner = { is_infertile_trigger = yes }
		}
		impregnate_effect_event_target = yes
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_witch_hunter_events, WHG_chaos_cult_events +6 more
increase_visibility_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = { which = visibility_value value = 5 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = visibility_value value = 15 }
				NOR = {
					has_character_modifier = under_suspicion
					has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
				}
			}
			add_character_modifier = {
				name = under_suspicion
				duration = -1
			}
			character_event = { id = society.61 days = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { which = visibility_value value = 25 }
				NOT = {
					has_character_modifier = highly_suspect
				}
			}
			remove_character_modifier = under_suspicion
			add_character_modifier = {
				name = highly_suspect duration = -1
			}
			character_event = { id = society.62 days = 1 }
			liege = {
				set_character_flag = secret_societies_interference
				character_event = { id = society.60 days = 1095 random = 1095 }
			}
		}
		if = { # Too visible? You auto-reveal yourself if you are independent
			limit = {
				independent = yes
				is_ruler = yes
				check_variable = { which = visibility_value value = 40 }
				NOT = {
					has_character_flag = self_reveal
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = self_reveal
			character_event = { id = society.65 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_bloodline_events, WHG_daemon_child_events +1 more
transfer_crown_on_abdication_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_landed_title = e_nehekhara
			any_artifact = { artifact_type = crown_of_nehekhara }
		}
			current_heir = { save_event_target_as = crown_hre_heir }
			any_artifact = {
				limit = { artifact_type = crown_of_nehekhara }
				transfer_artifact = {
					from = PREV
					to = event_target:crown_hre_heir
				}
			}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_landed_title = k_saphery
			any_artifact = { artifact_type = war_crown_of_saphery }
		}
			current_heir = { save_event_target_as = crown_byzantine_heir }
			any_artifact = {
				limit = { artifact_type = war_crown_of_saphery }
				transfer_artifact = {
					from = PREV
					to = event_target:crown_byzantine_heir
				}
			}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_artifact = { artifact_type = shadow_crown_alith }
		}
			current_heir = { save_event_target_as = crown_pahlavi_heir }
			any_artifact = {
				limit = { artifact_type = shadow_crown_alith }
				transfer_artifact = {
					from = PREV
					to = event_target:crown_pahlavi_heir
				}
			}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_landed_title = e_ulthuan
			any_artifact = { artifact_type = phoenix_crown_new }
		}
			current_heir = { save_event_target_as = crown_fylkir_heir }
			any_artifact = {
				limit = { artifact_type = phoenix_crown_new }
				transfer_artifact = {
					from = PREV
					to = event_target:crown_fylkir_heir
				}
			}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_landed_title = e_karak_ankor
				has_landed_title = d_high_seat
			}
			any_artifact = { artifact_type = dragon_crown_karaz }
		}
			current_heir = { save_event_target_as = crown_tengri_fylkir_heir }
			any_artifact = {
				limit = { artifact_type = dragon_crown_karaz }
				transfer_artifact = {
					from = PREV
					to = event_target:crown_tengri_fylkir_heir
				}
			}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			trait = dwarf_slayer_king
			any_artifact = { artifact_type = slayer_crown }
		}
			current_heir = { save_event_target_as = crown_slavic_fylkir_heir }
			any_artifact = {
				limit = { artifact_type = slayer_crown }
				transfer_artifact = {
					from = PREV
					to = event_target:crown_slavic_fylkir_heir
				}
			}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_landed_title = e_bretonnia
			any_artifact = { artifact_type = bretonnia_crown }
		}
			current_heir = { save_event_target_as = crown_romuva_fylkir_heir }
			any_artifact = {
				limit = { artifact_type = bretonnia_crown }
				transfer_artifact = {
					from = PREV
					to = event_target:crown_romuva_fylkir_heir
				}
			}
	}
	#Now transfer all other remaining artifacts to the player_heir, who may or may not be the current_heir.
	player_heir = { save_event_target_as = scoped_player_heir }
	any_artifact = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				artifact_type = bretonnia_crown
				artifact_type = slayer_crown
				artifact_type = dragon_crown_karaz
				artifact_type = phoenix_crown_new
				artifact_type = shadow_crown_alith
				artifact_type = war_crown_of_saphery
				artifact_type = crown_of_nehekhara
			}
		}
		transfer_artifact = {
			from = PREV
			to = event_target:scoped_player_heir
		}
	}
}

# Used by: 00_cb_types, WHG_engine_effects, WHG_faith_effects
give_chaos_artifacts = {
	random_list = {
		#Crusade Weapons
		20 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_artifact = axe_of_the_fallen
					has_artifact = the_eternal_blade
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.05
				OR = {
					has_artifact = burning_blade
					has_artifact = chaos_artifact_hellfire_sword
					has_artifact = chaos_artifact_change_sword
					has_artifact = chaos_artifact_daemonblade
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = sword_tier_1
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = crusader_king_flag
				}
				add_artifact = the_eternal_blade
			}
			else = {
				add_artifact  = axe_of_the_fallen
			}
			clr_character_flag = sword_tier_1
		}
		20 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_artifact = burning_blade
					has_artifact = chaos_artifact_hellfire_sword
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.05
				OR = {
					has_artifact = axe_of_the_fallen
					has_artifact = the_eternal_blade
					has_artifact = chaos_artifact_change_sword
					has_artifact = chaos_artifact_daemonblade
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = lance_tier_2
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = crusader_king_flag
				}
				add_artifact = chaos_artifact_hellfire_sword
			}
			else = {
				add_artifact  = burning_blade
			}
			clr_character_flag = lance_tier_2
		}
		20 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				OR = {
					has_artifact = chaos_artifact_change_sword
					has_artifact = chaos_artifact_daemonblade
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.05
				OR = {
					has_artifact = axe_of_the_fallen
					has_artifact = the_eternal_blade
					has_artifact = burning_blade
					has_artifact = chaos_artifact_hellfire_sword
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = axe_tier_2
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = crusader_king_flag
				}
				add_artifact = chaos_artifact_daemonblade
			}
			else = {
				add_artifact  = chaos_artifact_change_sword
			}
			clr_character_flag = axe_tier_2
		}

		#Random Christian associated artifacts - changed to chaos
		5 = {
			add_artifact = daemon_heart
		}
		5 = {
			add_artifact = whispering_daemon_eye
		}
		5 = {
			add_artifact = daemon_blood
		}
		5 = {
			add_artifact = daemon_bone
		}
		5 = {
			add_artifact = daemon_tongue
		}
		5 = {
			add_artifact = worshipers_skull
		}
		#Crusade Armor
		20 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				has_artifact = rot_helm
				has_artifact = dragon_ogre_plate
			}
			set_character_flag = armor_tier_2
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character_flag = crusader_king_flag
				}
				add_artifact = dragon_ogre_plate
			}
			else = {
				add_artifact  = rot_helm
			}
			clr_character_flag = armor_tier_2
		}
		#Artifacts based on Saints created throughout the game - remvoed due to no Chaos saints
	}
}

# The doom counter is the variable global_doom_counter of province 1, where
# doom.1 (events/WHG_doom_events.txt) reads it each year. The add_ and
# reduce_ effects can be called from any scope; they switch to province 1.
# check_doom_not_over_max_effect and check_doom_not_negative_effect must
# run in province 1.
# Used by: WHG_daemon_events, WHG_chaos_dwarf_events +10 more
add_doom_progress_tiny_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = { text = chaos_increases_tt }
	hidden_effect = {
		1 = { # the counter lives in province 1 (doom.1)
			change_variable = {
				which = "global_doom_counter"
				value = 1
			}
			check_doom_not_over_max_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects, WHG_daemon_events, WHG_bulwark_events +10 more

	custom_tooltip = { text = chaos_increases_tt }
	hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = "global_doom_counter"
				value = 5
			}
			check_doom_not_over_max_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

				which = "global_doom_counter"

		}
	}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects, WHG_seduction_events +4 more
mutations_table = {
	random_list = {
		5 = {	#acid_excretion
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						trait = acid_excretion_1
						trait = acid_excretion_2
						trait = acid_excretion_3
					}
				}
				add_trait = acid_excretion_1
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = acid_excretion_1 }
				remove_trait = acid_excretion_1
				add_trait = acid_excretion_2
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = acid_excretion_2 }
				remove_trait = acid_excretion_2
				add_trait = acid_excretion_3
			}
			change_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 1 }
		}
		5 = {	#addiction
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						trait = addiction_1
						trait = addiction_2
						trait = addiction_3
						trait = addiction_4
					}
				}
				add_trait = addiction_1
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = addiction_1 }
				remove_trait = addiction_1
				add_trait = addiction_2
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = addiction_2 }
				remove_trait = addiction_2
				add_trait = addiction_3
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = addiction_3 }
				remove_trait = addiction_3
				add_trait = addiction_4
			}
			change_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 1 }
		}
		5 = {	#additional_eye
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						trait = cyclops
						trait = additional_eye_1
						trait = additional_eye_2
						trait = additional_eye_3
						trait = additional_eye_4
						trait = additional_eye_5
						trait = additional_eye_6
						trait = additional_eye_7
						trait = additional_eye_8
						trait = additional_eye_9
						trait = additional_eye_10
					}
				}
				add_trait = additional_eye_1
				remove_trait = one_eyed
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = additional_eye_1 }
				remove_trait = additional_eye_1
				add_trait = additional_eye_2
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = additional_eye_2 }
				remove_trait = additional_eye_2
				add_trait = additional_eye_3
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = additional_eye_3 }
				remove_trait = additional_eye_3
				add_trait = additional_eye_4
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = additional_eye_4 }
				remove_trait = additional_eye_4
				add_trait = additional_eye_5
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = additional_eye_5 }
				remove_trait = additional_eye_5
				add_trait = additional_eye_6
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = additional_eye_6 }
				remove_trait = additional_eye_6
				add_trait = additional_eye_7
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = additional_eye_7 }
				remove_trait = additional_eye_7
				add_trait = additional_eye_8
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = additional_eye_8 }
				remove_trait = additional_eye_8
				add_trait = additional_eye_9
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = additional_eye_9 }
				remove_trait = additional_eye_9
				add_trait = additional_eye_10
			}
			change_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 1 }
		}
		5 = {	#agile
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						trait = agile_1
						trait = agile_2

# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects, WHG_chaos_cult_events +3 more
chaos_spawn_effect = {
	abdicate = yes
	set_character_flag = do_not_disturb
	any_friend = { remove_friend = ROOT }
	any_rival = { remove_rival = ROOT }
	any_lover = { remove_lover = ROOT }
	any_spouse = { remove_spouse = ROOT }
	hidden_tooltip = {
		add_trait = disinherited
		remove_race_trait_effect = yes
		add_trait = creature_chaosspawn
		set_graphical_culture = chaosspawn
		culture = chaosspawn
		dynasty = none
	    recalc_succession = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group }
			}
			religion = chaos
		}
		banish = yes
		mutations_table = yes
	}
}

	if = {

				society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle
				follower_of_nurgle = yes
				culture = pestilens
				has_character_flag = plague_bearer
			}
		}
		character_event = { id = nurgle.20 } # WHG_chaos_gods_events
	}
}

# Creates the chieftain of a warherd: mostly an undivided Chaos warrior, else a
# Khornate berserker or a Slaaneshi, Nurglite or Tzeentchian (shaman) beastman.
# Use new_character afterwards to reach the chieftain.
# Scope: the province where the warherd rises.
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events
beastmen_warherd_chieftain_effect = {
	random_list = {
		65 = {
			create_character = {
				random_traits = yes
				dynasty = none
				religion = chaos
				culture = beastman_main
				female = no
				age = 23
				attributes = { martial = 7 }
				trait = creature_beastman
				trait = tough_soldier
				trait = horns_average
			}
		}
		20 = {
			create_character = {
				random_traits = yes
				dynasty = none
				religion = khorne
				culture = beastman_main
				female = no
				age = 23
				attributes = { martial = 9 }
				trait = creature_beastman
				trait = tough_soldier
				trait = berserker
				trait = horns_average
			}
		}
		5 = {
			create_character = {
				random_traits = yes
				dynasty = none
				religion = slaanesh
				culture = beastman_main
				female = no
				age = 23
				attributes = { intrigue = 5 }
				trait = creature_beastman
				trait = misguided_warrior
				trait = horns_average
			}
		}
		5 = {
			create_character = {
				random_traits = yes
				dynasty = none
				religion = nurgle
				culture = beastman_main
				female = no
				age = 23
				attributes = { diplomacy = 5 }
				trait = creature_beastman
				trait = misguided_warrior
				trait = horns_average
			}
		}
		5 = {
			create_character = {
				random_traits = yes
				dynasty = none
				religion = tzeentch
				culture = beastman_main
				female = no
				age = 23
				attributes = { learning = 5 }
				trait = creature_beastman
				trait = intricate_webweaver
				trait = lore_tzeentch
				trait = horns_average
			}
		}
	}
}

# The warherd declares war on the province's top liege.
# Scope: the chieftain. ROOT is the province.
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events
beastmen_warherd_war_effect = {
	ROOT = {
		owner = {
			top_liege = {
				reverse_war = {
					target = PREVPREVPREV
					casus_belli = beastmen_uprising
					thirdparty_title = ROOT # The county
				}
				reverse_opinion = { who = PREVPREVPREV modifier = opinion_prey }
			}
		}
	}
}

# Raiders butcher up to five courtiers who are not beastmen, each with a 75%
# chance. ROOT is the killer.
# Scope: the ruler whose court is raided.
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events
beastmen_slaughter_court_effect = {
	random_courtier = {
		limit = { beastmen_raid_victim_trigger = yes }
		set_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim
		random = { chance = 75 beastmen_slay_effect = yes }
	}
	random_courtier = {
		limit = { beastmen_raid_victim_trigger = yes }
		set_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim
		random = { chance = 75 beastmen_slay_effect = yes }
	}
	random_courtier = {
		limit = { beastmen_raid_victim_trigger = yes }
		set_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim
		random = { chance = 75 beastmen_slay_effect = yes }
	}
	random_courtier = {
		limit = { beastmen_raid_victim_trigger = yes }
		set_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim
		random = { chance = 75 beastmen_slay_effect = yes }
	}
	random_courtier = {
		limit = { beastmen_raid_victim_trigger = yes }
		set_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim
		random = { chance = 75 beastmen_slay_effect = yes }
	}
	any_courtier = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim }
		clr_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim
	}
}

# Raiders drag up to five courtiers who are not rulers or beastmen back to
# ROOT's court as prisoners (chances 100, 100, 90, 75 and 50%).
# Scope: the ruler whose court is raided.
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events
beastmen_take_captives_effect = {
	random_courtier = {
		limit = { is_ruler = no beastmen_raid_victim_trigger = yes }
		imprison = ROOT
		move_character = ROOT
	}
	random_courtier = {
		limit = { is_ruler = no beastmen_raid_victim_trigger = yes }
		imprison = ROOT
		move_character = ROOT
	}
	random_courtier = {
		limit = { is_ruler = no beastmen_raid_victim_trigger = yes }
		set_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim
		random = { chance = 90 imprison = ROOT move_character = ROOT }
	}
	random_courtier = {
		limit = { is_ruler = no beastmen_raid_victim_trigger = yes }
		set_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim
		random = { chance = 75 imprison = ROOT move_character = ROOT }
	}
	random_courtier = {
		limit = { is_ruler = no beastmen_raid_victim_trigger = yes }
		set_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim
		random = { chance = 50 imprison = ROOT move_character = ROOT }
	}
	any_courtier = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim }
		clr_character_flag = beastmen_raid_victim
	}
}

# A herdstone ritual or a scourging leaves its mark: the scourging level of
# the province goes up one step (1 to 3).
# Scope: the province.
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events
beastmen_scourge_province_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_province_modifier = beastmen_scourging_2 }
		remove_province_modifier = beastmen_scourging_2
		add_province_modifier = { name = beastmen_scourging_3 duration = 4000 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_province_modifier = beastmen_scourging_1 }
		remove_province_modifier = beastmen_scourging_1
		add_province_modifier = { name = beastmen_scourging_2 duration = 3600 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_province_modifier = beastmen_scourging_3 } }
		add_province_modifier = { name = beastmen_scourging_1 duration = 3200 }
	}
}

# The marshal's work is done: the province becomes beastman land with a
# herdstone. The capital turns nomadic, one other holding is razed and the
# scourging ends.
# Scope: the province.
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events
beastmen_raise_herdstone_effect = {
	capital_holding = { convert_to = NOMAD }
	random_province_holding = { limit = { is_capital = no } destroy_settlement = THIS }
	remove_province_modifier = beastmen_scourging_1
	remove_province_modifier = beastmen_scourging_2
	remove_province_modifier = beastmen_scourging_3
	add_province_modifier = { name = beastman_herdstone duration = -1 }
	add_province_modifier = { name = province_scourged duration = 4650 }
}

# Challenges the liege to a duel for the herd through the duel engine
# (duel.1), and tells the liege (beastmen.21). Moved here unchanged from
# decision duel_beastman_decision so beastmen.23 can use it too.
# Scope: the challenger.
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events, wh_beastmen_decisions
beastmen_challenge_liege_effect = {
	add_character_modifier = {
		modifier = recent_duel_timer
		duration = 720
		hidden = yes
	}
	save_event_target_as = combatant_1
	set_character_flag = duel
	set_character_flag = flag_duel_no_draws
	set_character_flag = deadly_duel@event_target:combatant_2
	liege = {
		save_event_target_as = combatant_2
		set_character_flag = duel
		set_character_flag = flag_duel_no_draws
		character_event = { id = beastmen.21 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = duel.1 } }
	}
}

# Declares a beastman_migration war on the de jure liege of a civilised
# province next to the realm. Check beastmen_has_prey_trigger first.
# Scope: the beastman ruler (ROOT).
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events
beastmen_attack_prey_effect = {
	random_realm_province = {
		limit = {
			any_neighbor_province = {
				owner = { NOT = { trait = creature_beastman } NOT = { is_liege_or_above = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		random_neighbor_province = {
			limit = {
				owner = { NOT = { trait = creature_beastman } NOT = { is_liege_or_above = ROOT } }
			}
			dejure_liege_title = {
				owner = {
					ROOT = {
						war = {
							casus_belli = beastman_migration
							target = PREV
							thirdparty_title = PREVPREV
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Uses up one road event and rolls the next one (or the arrival) in two to
# three months.
# Scope: the pilgrim.
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events
brayherd_return_to_road_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		subtract_variable = { which = brayherd_road_events value = 1 }
		character_event = { id = brayherd.3 days = 60 random = 30 }
	}
}

# Uses up one event at the Heart of the Dark and rolls the next one (or the
# rite at the Herdstone) in about six weeks.
# Scope: the pilgrim.
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events
brayherd_return_to_heart_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		subtract_variable = { which = brayherd_heart_events value = 1 }
		character_event = { id = brayherd.30 days = 40 random = 10 }
	}
}

# Ends the pilgrimage: the pilgrim is home, can be disturbed again and can
# never make the journey a second time. Clears every flag and variable the
# journey used.
# Scope: the pilgrim.
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events
brayherd_pilgrimage_end_effect = {
	remove_trait = travelling
	set_character_flag = brayherd_pilgrimage_done
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_on_pilgrimage
	clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_raided
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_challenged
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_met_malagor
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_met_taurox
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_met_centigors
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_caught_knight
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_was_hosted
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_hunted
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_befriended_hunter
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_killed_hunter
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_saw_shrine
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_fought_ring
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_saw_market
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_rite_bad
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_rite_okay
	clr_character_flag = brayherd_rite_great
	set_variable = { which = brayherd_road_events value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = brayherd_heart_events value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = brayherd_herdstone_favour value = 0 }
}

# Gives a travelling trader one beastman artifact the pilgrim does not own
# yet, saved as event_target:brayherd_trader_artifact.

			add_artifact = beastmen_artifact_dark_rain_chalice
		}

			add_artifact = beastmen_artifact_ramhorn_helm
		}

			add_artifact = beastmen_artifact_fur_sharrgu
		}
		10 = {

			add_artifact = beastmen_artifact_great_fang

		10 = {
			trigger = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_artifact = beastmen_artifact_stonecrusher_mace } } }
			add_artifact = beastmen_artifact_stonecrusher_mace
		}
		10 = {
			trigger = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_artifact = beastmen_artifact_black_maul } } }
			add_artifact = beastmen_artifact_black_maul
		}
		5 = {
			trigger = { ROOT = { NOT = { has_artifact = beastmen_artifact_chaos_armour } } }
			add_artifact = beastmen_artifact_chaos_armour
		}
	}
	new_artifact = { save_event_target_as = brayherd_trader_artifact }
}

# Removes a trader (and whatever they still carry) from the game.
# Scope: the trader.
# Used by: WHG_beastmen_events
brayherd_trader_leaves_effect = {
	any_artifact = { destroy_artifact = yes }
	if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
	else = { death = { death_reason = death_missing } }
}

# Used by WHG_black_crusade_events
###############################################################################

# Used by: WHG_black_crusade_events, WHG_disease_events
decrease_depopulation_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_province_modifier = depopulated_1
		}
		remove_province_modifier = depopulated_1
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_province_modifier = depopulated_2
		}
		remove_province_modifier = depopulated_2
		add_province_modifier = {
			name = depopulated_1
			duration = -1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_province_modifier = depopulated_3
		}
		remove_province_modifier = depopulated_3
		add_province_modifier = {
			name = depopulated_2
			duration = -1
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_black_crusade_events
decrease_prosperity_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_province_modifier = prosperity_modifier_3
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			remove_province_modifier = prosperity_modifier_3
			set_variable = { which = prosperity_value value = 250 }
		}
		add_province_modifier = { name = prosperity_modifier_2 duration = -1 }
	}
}

# A lord who pledges adds prestige and piety to the war chest by rank.
# Scope: the pledging lord.
# Used by: WHG_black_crusade_events
bcrusade_pledge_pot_effect = {
	trigger_switch = {
		on_trigger = tier
		EMPEROR = { add_to_crusade_prestige_pot = 2000 add_to_crusade_piety_pot = 800 }
		KING = { add_to_crusade_prestige_pot = 1000 add_to_crusade_piety_pot = 400 }
		DUKE = { add_to_crusade_prestige_pot = 500 add_to_crusade_piety_pot = 200 }
		COUNT = { add_to_crusade_prestige_pot = 250 add_to_crusade_piety_pot = 100 }
		BARON = { add_to_crusade_prestige_pot = 125 add_to_crusade_piety_pot = 100 }
	}
}

# crusadefl.4 The merchant caravan: pick the piece of regalia the caravan
# offers, by the faith of the land and the commander's rank. Sets one
# crusadefl_ware_* flag. Scope: the commander, with event target
# current_location.
# Used by: WHG_black_crusade_events
crusadefl_caravan_pick_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:current_location = {
				OR = {
					religion_group = old_world_group
					religion_group = stromfels_group
					religion_group = halfling_gods_group
				}
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_crown_of_majesty }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_emerald_scepter }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_sword_of_heroes }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_crown_of_pearls }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_ruby_scepter }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_golden_sword }
			10 = { trigger = { NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } } set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_the_lily_crown }
			10 = { trigger = { NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } } set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_noble_scepter }
			10 = { trigger = { NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } } set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_engraved_sword }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:current_location = {
				OR = {
					religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
					religion_group = nehekharan_gods_group
					religion_group = old_ones_group
				}
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_dagger_of_glory }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_ruby_bracelets }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_bejeweled_dagger }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_golden_bracelets }
			10 = { trigger = { NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } } set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_engraved_dagger }
			10 = { trigger = { NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } } set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_silver_bracelets }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:current_location = {
				OR = {
					religion_group = cadai_group
					religion_group = cytharai_group
				}
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_circlet_of_the_heavens }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_staff_of_majesty }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_circlet_of_the_sun }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_golden_staff }
			10 = { trigger = { NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } } set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_circlet_of_twilight }
			10 = { trigger = { NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } } set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_silver_staff }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:current_location = {
				religion_group = oriental_gods_group
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_celestial_necklace }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_necklace_of_radiance }
			10 = { trigger = { NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } } set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_collar_of_pearls }
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_crown_of_crows }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_crown_of_the_sea }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_heros_crown }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_axe_of_the_ancestors }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_amber_crown }
			10 = { set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_obsidian_axe }
			10 = { trigger = { NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } } set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_iron_crown }
			10 = { trigger = { NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } } set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_engraved_crown }
			10 = { trigger = { NOT = { higher_tier_than = DUKE } } set_character_flag = crusadefl_ware_ceremonial_axe }
		}
	}
}

# crusadefl.4: give the piece picked above, once a lifetime.

		on_trigger = has_character_flag

# Used by WHG_carnival_events

# The jester's touch: the character may catch the Red Pox, the Green Pox or,
# rarely, Nurgle's Rot. Uses the same flags as the disease events, so the
# symptoms develop as usual.
# Scope: a character.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_infect_effect = {
	random_list = {
		50 = { }
		22 = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_typhus }
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = has_typhus }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						has_character_flag = developing_illness
						has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = got_typhus
				set_character_flag = developing_illness
				character_event = { id = symptom.18 days = 14 random = 14 }
			}
		}
		22 = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_flag = immune_measles }
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = has_measles }
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						has_character_flag = developing_illness
						has_character_flag = disease_not_serious
					}
				}
				set_character_flag = got_measles
				set_character_flag = developing_illness
				character_event = { id = symptom.19 days = 14 random = 14 }
			}
		}
		6 = {
			modifier = { factor = 0 trait = has_nurgles_rot }
			add_trait = has_nurgles_rot hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { is_nurgle_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = has_nurgles_rot } }
			nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Picks who keeps an eye on the troupe: a court witch hunter, wizard or
# other seer if there is one, else the spymaster, else the ruler.
# Scope: the host.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_pick_watcher_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_courtier_or_vassal = { carnival_seer_trigger = yes prisoner = no is_adult = yes }
		}
		random_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { carnival_seer_trigger = yes prisoner = no is_adult = yes }
			save_global_event_target_as = carnival_watcher
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { job_spymaster = { prisoner = no } }
		job_spymaster = { save_global_event_target_as = carnival_watcher }
	}
	else = { save_global_event_target_as = carnival_watcher }
}

# Clears the host's chain flags once the Carnival has gone. carnival_visited
# stays, so the Carnival never comes back to the same ruler.
# Scope: the host.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_clear_host_effect = {
	clr_character_flag = carnival_host
	clr_character_flag = carnival_watched
	clr_character_flag = carnival_truth_seen
	clr_character_flag = carnival_forewarned
	clr_character_flag = carnival_master_noticed
	clr_character_flag = carnival_omen_11
	clr_character_flag = carnival_omen_12
	clr_character_flag = carnival_omen_13
	clr_character_flag = carnival_omen_14
	clr_character_flag = carnival_paid_blades
	clr_character_flag = carnival_ascension
	set_variable = { which = carnival_omens value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = carnival_risk value = 0 }
	set_variable = { which = carnival_hunt_steps value = 0 }
}

# The town the Carnival played in is left half empty and sick: a long plague
# modifier, and sometimes a true epidemic breaks out.
# Scope: the town (a province).
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_town_ravaged_effect = {
	add_province_modifier = { name = carnival_plague_town duration = 1825 }
	random_list = {
		50 = { }
		25 = { spawn_disease = typhus }
		25 = { spawn_disease = measles }
	}
}

# The Master takes a finger from a woman or child of the court and leads them
# away. Never a member of the host's own dynasty.
# Scope: the host.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_taken_effect = {
	random_courtier = {
		limit = {
			is_ruler = no
			prisoner = no
			NOT = { dynasty = ROOT }
			NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
			OR = { is_female = yes is_adult = no }
		}
		save_event_target_as = carnival_taken
		death = { death_reason = death_vanished }
	}
}

# At sunset the glamour slips: the Master shows his true faith and gifts.
# Scope: the Master.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_reveal_master_effect = {
	religion = nurgle
	if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = lore_nurgle } } add_trait = lore_nurgle }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = mark_nurgle } } add_trait = mark_nurgle }
}

# The ruler takes Grandfather's blessing and becomes the Carnival's patron.
# An Order ruler falls to Nurgle in secret. Raises the doom. Once in a life
# the Master leaves a Plague Cart behind as a gift.
# Scope: the ruler.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_patron_effect = {
	set_character_flag = carnival_patron
	save_global_event_target_as = carnival_patron
	add_doom_progress_minor_effect = yes
	if = { limit = { follower_of_nurgle = no } set_secret_religion = nurgle }
	add_character_modifier = { name = carnival_grandfathers_favour years = 10 }
	if = {
		limit = { nurgle_offmap_enabled_trigger = yes }
		add_offmap_currency = { offmap = nurgle_warp amount = 150 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = carnival_got_cart } }
		random = {
			chance = 50
			set_character_flag = carnival_got_cart
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = carnival_plague_cart_tt
				capital_scope = {
					PREV = {
						spawn_unit = {
							province = PREV
							owner = PREV
							troops = { daemonic_host = { 100 100 } light_infantry = { 150 150 } }
							earmark = carnival_plague_cart
							maintenance_multiplier = 0.5
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# The Master is dead: the doom falls, and once in a life there is a reward.
# One in three takes his mask (if the ruler has none), else one in three
# brings a band of plague-hunters to the ruler's banner.
# Scope: the ruler who ended the Carnival.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_master_slain_effect = {
	reduce_doom_progress_minor_effect = yes
	add_character_modifier = { name = carnival_ended_the_dance years = 10 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character_flag = carnival_got_reward }
			NOT = { has_artifact = carnival_master_mask }
		}
		random_list = {
			33 = { set_character_flag = carnival_got_reward add_artifact = carnival_master_mask }
			33 = {
				set_character_flag = carnival_got_reward
				custom_tooltip = {
					text = carnival_plague_hunters_tt
					capital_scope = {
						PREV = {
							spawn_unit = {
								province = PREV
								owner = PREV
								troops = {
									heavy_infantry = { 100 100 }
									light_infantry = { 100 100 }
								}
								earmark = carnival_plague_hunters
								maintenance_multiplier = 0.5
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			34 = { }
		}
	}
}

# The Master's troupe: a Tainted One (a jester who spreads the Rot with a
# handshake) and a Brute (a hooded, half-rotted strongman). Nurglite humans
# of the host's own people, secretly sworn to Nurgle until sunset.
# Scope: the first host.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_create_troupe_effect = {
	create_character = {
		random_traits = no
		dynasty = none
		religion = ROOT
		culture = ROOT
		female = no
		age = 28
		attributes = { diplomacy = 12 martial = 4 stewardship = 3 intrigue = 12 learning = 6 }
		trait = gregarious
		trait = deceitful
		trait = cynical
		flag = ai_flag_refuse_conversion
	}
	new_character = {
		save_global_event_target_as = carnival_jester
		set_character_flag = carnival_troupe
		add_trait = cannot_marry
		set_secret_religion = nurgle
		give_nickname = nick_the_tainted
	}
	create_character = {
		random_traits = no
		dynasty = none
		religion = ROOT
		culture = ROOT
		female = no
		age = 35
		attributes = { diplomacy = 2 martial = 10 stewardship = 2 intrigue = 2 learning = 1 }
		trait = strong
		trait = wroth
		trait = brave
		flag = ai_flag_refuse_conversion
	}
	new_character = {
		save_global_event_target_as = carnival_brute
		set_character_flag = carnival_troupe
		add_trait = cannot_marry
		set_secret_religion = nurgle
		give_nickname = nick_the_brute
	}
}

# Sunset: the Tainted One and the Brute show their faith, their sores and a
# gift of Nurgle each, and a Plaguebearer steps out of the Plague Cart.
# Scope: the host.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_reveal_troupe_effect = {
	event_target:carnival_jester = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = yes }
			religion = nurgle
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = mark_nurgle } } add_trait = mark_nurgle }
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = has_nurgles_rot } } add_trait = has_nurgles_rot hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { is_nurgle_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = has_nurgles_rot } } }
			mutations_table_nurgle = yes
		}
	}
	event_target:carnival_brute = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = yes }
			religion = nurgle
			if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = mark_nurgle } } add_trait = mark_nurgle }
			mutations_table_nurgle = yes
			mutations_table_nurgle = yes
		}
	}
	create_character = {
		random_traits = no
		dynasty = none
		religion = nurgle
		culture = plaguebearer_nurgle
		female = no
		age = 300
		attributes = { diplomacy = 3 martial = 8 stewardship = 15 intrigue = 4 learning = 6 }
		flag = ai_flag_refuse_conversion
	}
	new_character = {
		save_global_event_target_as = carnival_plaguebearer
		set_character_flag = carnival_troupe
		create_plaguebearer_effect = yes
	}
}

# The sunset is lost (or the gates were sealed): sometimes the Master
# ascends to Daemon Prince (carnival.33), else the town is lost
# (carnival.32).
# Scope: the host.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_defeat_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = carnival_ascension }
		character_event = { id = carnival.33 }
	}
	else = { character_event = { id = carnival.32 } }
}

# The Plaguebearer goes back into the Cart.
# Scope: any.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events, WHG_chaos_effects
carnival_banish_daemons_effect = {
	event_target:carnival_plaguebearer = {
		if = { limit = { is_alive = yes } death = { death_reason = death_vanished } }
	}
}

# The troupe follows the Master to the next host.
# Scope: the new host.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_move_troupe_effect = {
	event_target:carnival_jester = {
		if = { limit = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no } move_character = PREV }
	}
	event_target:carnival_brute = {
		if = { limit = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no } move_character = PREV }
	}
}

# The Carnival leaves the map: whoever of the troupe still walks free goes
# with it.
# Scope: any.
# Used by: WHG_carnival_events
carnival_troupe_vanish_effect = {
	event_target:carnival_jester = {
		if = { limit = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no } death = { death_reason = death_vanished } }
	}
	event_target:carnival_brute = {
		if = { limit = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no } death = { death_reason = death_vanished } }
	}
	carnival_banish_daemons_effect = yes
}

# Used by: WHG_carnival_events, WHG_doom_events
add_doom_prince_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_realm_province = {
				NOT = { region = world_chaos_wastes }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			25 = {
				add_doom_progress_minor_effect = yes
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.3
					is_landed = no
				}
			}
			25 = {
				add_doom_progress_tiny_effect = yes
			}
			50 = { }
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_list = {
			5 = {
				add_doom_progress_minor_effect = yes
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.3
					is_landed = no
				}
			}
			45 = {
				add_doom_progress_tiny_effect = yes
			}
			50 = { }
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events, WHG_chaos_effects
give_has_tuberculosis_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { trait = has_tuberculosis } }

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc = "Reapers"
				NOT = { has_character_flag = developing_illness }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = disease_not_serious }
			}
			set_character_flag = got_tuberculosis
			set_character_flag = developing_illness
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = symptom.16 days = 3 random = 40 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } }
			add_trait = has_tuberculosis hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = has_tuberculosis } }
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events, WHG_chaos_effects
give_dysentery_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { trait = dysentery }
			has_dlc = "Reapers"
			NOT = { has_character_flag = developing_illness }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = disease_not_serious }
		}

		set_character_flag = got_dysentery
		set_character_flag = developing_illness
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = symptom.22 days = 3 random = 10 } }
	}
	else_if = { # without Reaper's Due: plain illness
		limit = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } }
		give_ill_effect = yes
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
add_prolong_life_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOR = {
				has_character_modifier = stolen_life_minor
				has_character_modifier = stolen_life_medium
				has_character_modifier = stolen_life_major
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					name = stolen_life_minor
					days = 3650
				}
			}
			10 = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					name = stolen_life_medium
					days = 3650
				}
			}
			10 = {
				add_character_modifier = {
					name = stolen_life_major
					days = 3650
				}
			}
		}
		break = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_modifier = stolen_life_minor
				has_character_modifier = stolen_life_medium
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			remove_character_modifier = stolen_life_minor
			remove_character_modifier = stolen_life_medium
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
					name = stolen_life_major
					days = 3650
		}
		break = yes
	}
	add_character_modifier = {
		name = stolen_life_major
		days = 3650
	}
}

# Ends a recruitment: clears the flags that say which cult is courting the
# character.
# Scope: the recruit.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
chaoscult_clear_recruit_flags_effect = {
	clr_character_flag = being_recruited_by_khorne
	clr_character_flag = being_recruited_by_nurgle
	clr_character_flag = being_recruited_by_tzeentch
	clr_character_flag = being_recruited_by_slaanesh
	clr_character_flag = being_recruited_by_undivided
}

# Finds the grand master of the recruiter's cult and saves them as
# event_target:grand_master (used for the letter about how a recruitment
# went, chaoscult.49 and 50).
# Scope: any. Needs event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
chaoscult_find_grand_master_effect = {
	event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter = {
		random_society_member = {
			limit = { is_society_grandmaster = yes }
			save_event_target_as = grand_master
		}
	}
}

# No fitting cultist could come to meet the recruit (chaoscult.37), so the
# cult sends a new one: a servant shaped by their god, who joins the cult and
# takes the recruit's own secret faith. Saves them as
# event_target:chaos_cult_inductor.
# Scope: the new character. ROOT is the recruit.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
chaoscult_new_inductor_effect = {
	character_event = { id = aiclean.31 } # Set racial trait
	if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = being_recruited_by_khorne } }
		add_trait = strong
		add_trait = skilled_tactician
		join_society = cult_of_khorne
		set_secret_religion = khorne
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = being_recruited_by_nurgle } }
		add_trait = pneumonic hidden_effect = { if = { limit = { is_disease_immune_race = yes } remove_trait = pneumonic } }
		nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
		add_trait = fortune_builder
		join_society = cult_of_nurgle
		set_secret_religion = nurgle
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = being_recruited_by_tzeentch } }
		add_trait = beak
		add_trait = elusive_shadow
		join_society = cult_of_tzeentch
		set_secret_religion = tzeentch
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { has_character_flag = being_recruited_by_slaanesh } }
		remove_trait = ugly
		remove_trait = hunchback
		remove_trait = dwarf
		remove_trait = chaste
		add_trait = lustful
		add_trait = fair
		add_trait = elusive_shadow
		join_society = cult_of_slaanesh
		set_secret_religion = slaanesh
	}
	else = {
		add_trait = beak
		add_trait = scholarly_theologian
		join_society = cult_of_undivided
		set_secret_religion = chaos
	}
	save_event_target_as = chaos_cult_inductor
}

# The victim on the altar (FROM) may be the one a sacrifice mission asked
# for: a virgin, a holy person, a dwarf or a ruler. If so, clears that mission,
# plays its sound and sets chaoscult_sacrifice_for_quest so the event pays the
# mission reward instead of the plain one.
# Scope: the sacrificer.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
chaoscult_sacrifice_quest_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_quest = quest_the_devil_worshipers_sacrifice_virgin
			FROM = { is_virgin_trigger = yes }
		}
		clr_quest = quest_the_devil_worshipers_sacrifice_virgin
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_female = yes } }
			sound_effect = devil_worshippers_sacrifice_virgin_female
		}
		else = {
			sound_effect = devil_worshippers_sacrifice_virgin_male
		}
		set_character_flag = chaoscult_sacrifice_for_quest
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_quest = quest_the_devil_worshipers_sacrifice_holy_person
			FROM = { OR = { is_priest = yes is_ascetic_trigger = yes } }
		}
		clr_quest = quest_the_devil_worshipers_sacrifice_holy_person
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_female = yes } }
			sound_effect = devil_worshippers_sacrifice_holy_woman
		}
		else = {
			sound_effect = devil_worshippers_sacrifice_holy_man
		}
		set_character_flag = chaoscult_sacrifice_for_quest
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_quest = quest_the_devil_worshipers_sacrifice_dwarf
			FROM = { trait = dwarf }
		}
		clr_quest = quest_the_devil_worshipers_sacrifice_dwarf
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_female = yes } }
			sound_effect = devil_worshippers_sacrifice_dwarf_female
		}
		else = {
			sound_effect = devil_worshippers_sacrifice_dwarf_male
		}
		set_character_flag = chaoscult_sacrifice_for_quest
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_quest = quest_the_devil_worshipers_sacrifice_ruler
			FROM = { is_landed = yes }
		}
		clr_quest = quest_the_devil_worshipers_sacrifice_ruler
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_female = yes } }
			sound_effect = devil_worshippers_sacrifice_ruler_female
		}
		else = {
			sound_effect = devil_worshippers_sacrifice_ruler_male
		}
		set_character_flag = chaoscult_sacrifice_for_quest
	}
}

# Scope: the hunted cultist. The hunters kill him: a Sister of Sigmar
# impales him, a lone witch hunter takes his time, the rest burn him.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
chaoscult_hunt_execution_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
		antideath_effect = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = cult_hunt_sister
				has_character_flag = cult_hunt_sister_again
			}
		}
		death = { death_reason = death_execution_impaling }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_character_flag = cult_hunt_hunter
				has_character_flag = cult_hunt_hunter_again
			}
		}
		death = { death_reason = death_execution_sawing }
	}
	else = {
		death = { death_reason = death_execution_burning }
	}
}

# Scope: the flawed cultist. Clears the flags naming the flaw.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
chaoscult_clear_flaw_flags_effect = {
	clr_character_flag = chaoscult_too_honest
	clr_character_flag = chaoscult_too_wroth
	clr_character_flag = chaoscult_too_slothful
	clr_character_flag = chaoscult_too_trusting
	clr_character_flag = chaoscult_too_indiscreet
}

# Scope: the flawed cultist; FROM the teacher. The lessons work half the
# time, more with the teacher's intrigue: the teacher hears of it
# (chaoscult.458 or 459) and tells the student.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
chaoscult_flaw_lessons_effect = {
	random_list = {
		50 = {
			modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 12 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 15 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 18 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.2 FROM = { intrigue = 21 } }
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPchaoscult457_success }
			hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chaoscult.458 days = 21 } } }
			tooltip = {
				trigger_switch = {
					on_trigger = has_character_flag
					chaoscult_too_honest = { remove_trait = honest }
					chaoscult_too_wroth = { remove_trait = wroth }
					chaoscult_too_slothful = { remove_trait = slothful }
					chaoscult_too_trusting = { remove_trait = trusting }
					chaoscult_too_indiscreet = { change_intrigue = 2 }
				}
			}
		}
		50 = {
			custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPchaoscult457_failure }
			hidden_effect = { FROM = { character_event = { id = chaoscult.459 days = 21 } } }
		}
	}
}

# Chaos corruption of the soul (restored vanilla soul corruption, MNM.7050).

# (chaoscult.490 - 491), and may, rarely, open the way to possession
# (chaoscult.493 - 495).

major_chaos_corruption_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = {
		text = major_chaos_corruption_effect_tooltip
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		character_event = { id = chaoscult.490 days = 3 random = 27 }
	}
}

# The trait changes of a major corruption, per god (chaoscult.490).
# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
chaos_corruption_major_roll_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				society_member_of = cult_of_khorne
			}
			random_list = {
				20 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								trait = kind
							}
							trait = cruel
						}
					}
					remove_trait = kind
					add_trait = cruel
				}

					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						trait = wroth
					}
					remove_trait = kind
					add_trait = wroth
				}

					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						OR = {
							NOT = {
								trait = weak
							}
							trait = strong
						}
					}
					remove_trait = weak
					add_trait = strong
				}

						text = mutations_test_tt

# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
join_cult_as_member_or_sleeper_agent = {
	trigger_switch = {
		on_trigger = has_character_flag
		being_recruited_by_khorne = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter = {
							ai = no
						}
						can_join_society = cult_of_khorne
						ai = no
					}
					OR = {
						ai = no
						is_in_society = no
					}
				}
				join_society = cult_of_khorne
			}
			else = {
				event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter = {
					set_character_flag = recruitment_success_sleeper_agent
				}
				set_character_flag = chaos_cult_sleeper_agent
			}
			set_secret_religion = khorne
		}
		being_recruited_by_nurgle = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter = {
							ai = no
						}
						can_join_society = cult_of_nurgle
						ai = no
					}
					OR = {
						ai = no
						is_in_society = no
					}
				}
				join_society = cult_of_nurgle
			}
			else = {
				event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter = {
					set_character_flag = recruitment_success_sleeper_agent
				}
				set_character_flag = chaos_cult_sleeper_agent
			}
			set_secret_religion = nurgle
		}
		being_recruited_by_tzeentch = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter = {
							ai = no
						}
						can_join_society = cult_of_tzeentch
						ai = no
					}
					OR = {
						ai = no
						is_in_society = no
					}
				}
				join_society = cult_of_tzeentch
			}
			else = {
				event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter = {
					set_character_flag = recruitment_success_sleeper_agent
				}
				set_character_flag = chaos_cult_sleeper_agent
			}
			set_secret_religion = tzeentch
		}
		being_recruited_by_slaanesh = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter = {
							ai = no
						}
						can_join_society = cult_of_slaanesh
						ai = no
					}
					OR = {
						ai = no
						is_in_society = no
					}
				}
				join_society = cult_of_slaanesh
			}
			else = {
				event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter = {
					set_character_flag = recruitment_success_sleeper_agent
				}
				set_character_flag = chaos_cult_sleeper_agent
			}
			set_secret_religion = slaanesh
		}
		being_recruited_by_undivided = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter = {
							ai = no
						}
						can_join_society = cult_of_undivided
						ai = no
					}
					OR = {
						ai = no
						is_in_society = no
					}
				}
				join_society = cult_of_undivided
			}
			else = {
				event_target:chaos_cult_recruiter = {
					set_character_flag = recruitment_success_sleeper_agent
				}
				set_character_flag = chaos_cult_sleeper_agent
			}
			set_secret_religion = chaos
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
create_cultists_crossing_border = {
	random_independent_ruler = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				religion_group = old_world_group
				religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
				religion_group = oriental_gods_group
			}
			is_human = yes
			is_landed = yes
			higher_tier_than = BARON
			is_feudal = yes
			NOT = {
				government = congregation_government
			}
			OR = {
				shares_realm_border_with = event_target:chaos_ruler
				any_realm_province = {
					event_target:chaos_ruler = {
						any_realm_province = {
							can_naval_path_to = {
								target = PREVPREV
								distance < 300
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		save_event_target_as = the_target
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:the_target = {
				character = no
			}
		}
		break = yes
	}
	event_target:chaos_ruler = {
		set_variable = {
			which = cultists_to_move
			value = 5
		}
		while = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = cultists_to_move
					value > 0
				}
			}
			subtract_variable = {
				which = cultists_to_move
				value = 1
			}
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					is_human = yes
					is_adult = yes
					open_follower_of_any_chaos_gods = yes
					is_unimportant_trigger = yes
					is_chaos_champion_chosen_or_prince_trigger = no
					is_in_society = no
					is_sick_or_injured_trigger = no
					is_ill = no
					is_incapable = no
				}
				preferred_limit = {
					is_attractive_trigger = yes
					is_smart_trigger = yes
					is_strong_trigger = yes
				}
				set_character_flag = chaos_cult_from@event_target:chaos_ruler
				save_event_target_as = chosen_cultist
				event_target:the_target = {
					random_realm_lord = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								religion_group = old_world_group
								religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
								religion_group = oriental_gods_group
							}
							is_human = yes
						}
						preferred_limit = {
							OR = {
								has_focus = focus_seduction
								has_focus = focus_carousing
							}
							NOT = {
								secret_religion_group = chaos_gods_group
							}
						}
						event_target:chosen_cultist = {
							move_character = PREV
							religion = PREV
							culture = PREV
							try_join_random_society = yes
						}
					}
				}
				clear_event_target = chosen_cultist
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			which = cultists_to_move
			value = 0
		}
	}
	event_target:the_target = {
		random_list = {
			35 = {
				any_realm_character = {
					limit = {
						has_character_flag = chaos_cult_from@event_target:chaos_ruler
					}
					set_character_flag = chaos_cult_infiltrator_successful
				}
			}
			15 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					has_character_modifier = expelled_k_witch_hunters_order
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.2
					NOT = {
						realm_intrigue = 15
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.2
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							has_job_title = job_spymaster
							secret_religion_group = chaos_gods_group
						}
					}
					realm_intrigue = 15
					NOT = {
						realm_intrigue = 20
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.5
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							has_job_title = job_spymaster
							secret_religion_group = chaos_gods_group
						}
					}
					realm_intrigue = 20
					NOT = {
						realm_intrigue = 25
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							has_job_title = job_spymaster
							secret_religion_group = chaos_gods_group
						}
					}
					realm_intrigue = 25
					NOT = {
						realm_intrigue = 30
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 2.5
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							has_job_title = job_spymaster
							secret_religion_group = chaos_gods_group
						}
					}
					realm_intrigue = 30
					NOT = {
						realm_intrigue = 35
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 3
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							has_job_title = job_spymaster
							secret_religion_group = chaos_gods_group
						}
					}
					realm_intrigue = 35
					NOT = {
						realm_intrigue = 40
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 4
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							has_job_title = job_spymaster
							secret_religion_group = chaos_gods_group
						}
					}
					realm_intrigue = 40
					NOT = {
						realm_intrigue = 50
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 6
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							has_job_title = job_spymaster
							secret_religion_group = chaos_gods_group
						}
					}
					realm_intrigue = 50
				}
				character_event = {
					id = chaoscult.421
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_cult_events
burn_captured_infiltrator = {
	any_realm_character = {
		limit = {
			has_character_flag = chaos_cult_from@event_target:chaos_ruler
			NOT = {
				has_character_flag = chaos_cult_infiltrator_successful
			}
		}
		add_trait = staked
		death = {
			show_portrait = yes
			death_reason = death_execution_burning
			killer = ROOT
		}
	}
}

# ROOT's temptation by the Blood God is over, either way: the path flags go
# (the beaten-path flags stay).
# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects, WHG_chaos_gods_events

	clr_character_flag = khorne_path_proof
	clr_character_flag = khorne_path_scars
	clr_character_flag = khorne_path_hunt
	clr_character_flag = khorne_path_skulls
	clr_character_flag = khorne_path_war_games
}

# Kills the scoped character, murdered by ROOT. Invincible characters survive.
# Scope: the victim.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects, WHG_beastmen_events
beastmen_slay_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
		antideath_effect = yes
	}
	else = {
		death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = ROOT }
	}
}

# A summoned daemon or champion binds itself to its new master.
# Scope: the new character. ROOT is the master.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects
darkgift_bond_effect = {
	add_friend = ROOT
	reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_darkgift_bound years = 100 }
	opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_servant months = 1200 }
	set_character_flag = no_court_invites
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects, cb_types_chaos
add_doom_progress_major_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = { text = chaos_increases_tt }
	hidden_effect = {
		1 = { # the counter lives in province 1 (doom.1)
			change_variable = {
				which = "global_doom_counter"
				value = 15
			}
			check_doom_not_over_max_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects
check_doom_not_over_max_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				which = "global_doom_counter"
				value > 1500
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			which = "global_doom_counter"
			value = 1500
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects
check_doom_not_negative_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					which = "global_doom_counter"
					value = 1
				}
			}
	   }
	   set_variable = { which = "global_doom_counter" value = 1 }
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects
mutations_table_nurgle = {
	random_list = {
		1 = {	#acid_excretion
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						trait = acid_excretion_1
						trait = acid_excretion_2
						trait = acid_excretion_3
					}
				}
				add_trait = acid_excretion_1
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = acid_excretion_1 }
				remove_trait = acid_excretion_1
				add_trait = acid_excretion_2
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = acid_excretion_2 }
				remove_trait = acid_excretion_2
				add_trait = acid_excretion_3
			}
			change_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 1 }
		}
		1 = {	#atrophy
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						trait = atrophy_1
						trait = atrophy_2
						trait = atrophy_3
						trait = atrophy_4
						trait = atrophy_5
					}
				}
				add_trait = atrophy_1
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = atrophy_1 }
				remove_trait = atrophy_1
				add_trait = atrophy_2
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = atrophy_2 }
				remove_trait = atrophy_2
				add_trait = atrophy_3
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = atrophy_3 }
				remove_trait = atrophy_3
				add_trait = atrophy_4
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { trait = atrophy_4 }
				remove_trait = atrophy_4
				add_trait = atrophy_5
			}
			change_variable = { which = "mutation_counter" value = 1 }
		}
		1 = {	#beast_with_a_thousand...
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						trait = beast_with_a_thousand_eyes
						trait = beast_with_a_thousand_noses
						trait = beast_with_a_thousand_ears
						trait = beast_with_a_thousand_sores
						trait = beast_with_a_thousand_tongues
						trait = beast_with_a_thousand_nipples
						trait = beast_with_a_thousand_arms
						trait = beast_with_a_thousand_legs
						trait = beast_with_a_thousand_hands
						trait = beast_with_a_thousand_feet
						trait = beast_with_a_thousand_fingers

# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects
mutations_table_list = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { true_religion_group = chaos_gods_group }
				follower_of_undivided = yes
				trait = forsaken
			}
		}
		mutations_table = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			follower_of_tzeentch = yes
			NOT = { trait = forsaken }
		}
		mutations_table_tzeentch = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			follower_of_nurgle = yes
			NOT = { trait = forsaken }
		}
		mutations_table_nurgle = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			follower_of_slaanesh = yes
			NOT = { trait = forsaken }
		}
		mutations_table_slaanesh = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			follower_of_khorne = yes
			NOT = { trait = forsaken }
		}
		mutations_table_khorne = yes
	}
}

# A kind act may soften a cruel ruler. Scope: a ruler.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects, WHG_chaos_undivided_events
ravenchild_mercy_effect = {
	if = { limit = { trait = cruel } random = { chance = 20 remove_trait = cruel } }
	if = {
		limit = { NOR = { trait = kind trait = cruel } }
		random = {
			chance = 20
			add_trait = kind
			hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = traitnote.268 } }
		}
	}
}

# Clears whatever stage the seeker was on and restarts the reminder count.
# Scope: the seeker.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects, WHG_chaos_undivided_events
undivided_quest_clear_stage_effect = {
	clr_character_flag = everchosen_stage_altar
	clr_character_flag = everchosen_stage_coast
	clr_character_flag = everchosen_stage_necropolis
	clr_character_flag = everchosen_stage_cliff
	clr_character_flag = everchosen_stage_hunt
	clr_character_flag = everchosen_stage_zanbaijin
	clr_character_flag = everchosen_busy
	remove_character_modifier = everchosen_quest_altar
	remove_character_modifier = everchosen_quest_coast
	remove_character_modifier = everchosen_quest_necropolis
	remove_character_modifier = everchosen_quest_cliff
	remove_character_modifier = everchosen_quest_hunt
	remove_character_modifier = everchosen_quest_zanbaijin
	set_variable = { which = everchosen_reminders value = 0 }
}

# The artifacts of the Everchosen vanish with their bearer, so the next
# seeker can win them again.
# Scope: the dead Everchosen or seeker.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects, WHG_chaos_undivided_events
undivided_destroy_artifacts_effect = {
	unsafe_destroy_artifact = armor_of_morkar
	unsafe_destroy_artifact = crown_of_domination
	unsafe_destroy_artifact = eye_of_sheerian
	unsafe_destroy_artifact = slayer_of_kings
	unsafe_destroy_artifact = steed_of_the_apocalypse_mount
}

# A guardian of a treasure grows harder to beat as the Winds of Chaos weaken:
# at very high winds the gods favour the seeker.
# Scope: the guardian.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_effects
undivided_winds_temper_guardian_effect = {
	add_five_duel_experience_modifier = yes
	add_five_duel_experience_modifier = yes
	if = {
		limit = { is_doom_very_high_trigger = no }
		add_five_duel_experience_modifier = yes
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Used by WHG_chaos_gods_events
###############################################################################

# Scope: the challenger (ROOT). Raises the foe of the current round of
# Khorne's trial (variable khorne_trial: 1 a young warrior of the
# challenger's people, 2 a Khornate berserker, 3 a Bloodletter) at the
# challenger's court and saves it as khorne_trial_foe.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
khorne_trial_foe_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = khorne_trial value = 3 } }
		create_character = {
			random_traits = yes
			religion = khorne
			culture = bloodletter_khorne
			female = no
			age = 300
			flag = ai_flag_refuse_conversion
			attributes = { martial = 18 }
		}
		new_character = {
			add_trait = khorne_demon
			add_trait = khorne_bloodletter
			add_trait = wroth
			add_trait = brave
			save_event_target_as = khorne_trial_foe
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = khorne_trial value = 2 } }
		create_random_soldier = {
			random_traits = yes
			dynasty = none
			religion = khorne
			culture = ROOT
			female = no
			age = 30
		}
		new_character = {
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 } # Racial trait
			add_trait = brave
			add_trait = wroth
			add_trait = strong
			add_trait = khorne_berserker
			change_martial = 2
			save_event_target_as = khorne_trial_foe
		}
	}
	else = {
		create_random_soldier = {
			random_traits = yes
			dynasty = none
			religion = khorne
			culture = ROOT
			female = no
			age = 16
		}
		new_character = {
			character_event = { id = aiclean.31 } # Racial trait
			add_trait = brave
			change_martial = 1
			save_event_target_as = khorne_trial_foe
		}
	}
}

# Scope: the challenger. Ends the trial for good.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
khorne_trial_end_effect = {
	set_character_flag = tested_by_Khorne
	set_variable = { which = khorne_trial value = 0 }
	event_target:khorne_trial_foe = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = yes
				trait = khorne_bloodletter
			}
			death = { death_reason = death_vanished } # Back to the Realm of Chaos
		}
	}
}

# Scope: the sufferer. Gains a trait of despair unless already held, and
# remembers which ones the despair gave (flag nurgle_gave_<trait>).
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
nurgle_despair_craven_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { trait = craven } }
		add_trait = craven
		set_character_flag = nurgle_gave_craven
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
nurgle_despair_wroth_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { trait = wroth } }
		add_trait = wroth
		set_character_flag = nurgle_gave_wroth
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
nurgle_despair_charitable_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { trait = charitable } }
		add_trait = charitable
		set_character_flag = nurgle_gave_charitable
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
nurgle_despair_depressed_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { trait = depressed } }
		add_trait = depressed
		set_character_flag = nurgle_gave_depressed
	}
}

# Scope: the sufferer. Takes back the traits the despair gave.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
nurgle_despair_lift_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = nurgle_gave_craven }
		remove_trait = craven
		clr_character_flag = nurgle_gave_craven
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = nurgle_gave_wroth }
		remove_trait = wroth
		clr_character_flag = nurgle_gave_wroth
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = nurgle_gave_charitable }
		remove_trait = charitable
		clr_character_flag = nurgle_gave_charitable
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = nurgle_gave_depressed }
		remove_trait = depressed
		clr_character_flag = nurgle_gave_depressed
	}
}

# Scope: the dreamer. Loses the dull wits the visions burn away.

	remove_trait = imbecile

# Scope: the dreamer. Loses every gift of the mind.

	remove_trait = brilliant
	remove_trait = quick
	remove_trait = smart
	remove_trait = bright
}

# ROOT is a follower of Nurgle with a disease they have not yet been blessed
# for. Grants the gift for the first such disease (once per disease per
# life, flag nb_<disease>); with none left, lifts sick_incapable.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
nurgle_blessing_gift_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			trait = ill
			NOT = { has_character_flag = nb_ill }
		}
		change_martial = 1
		health = 3
		fertility = 0.1
		set_character_flag = nb_ill
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			trait = pneumonic
			NOT = { has_character_flag = nb_pneumonic }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			add_twenty_five_duel_experience_modifier = yes
		}
		change_diplomacy = 2
		change_stewardship = 2
		change_martial = 2
		change_intrigue = 2
		change_learning = 2
		health = 5
		fertility = 0.5
		set_character_flag = nb_pneumonic
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			trait = syphilitic
			NOT = { has_character_flag = nb_syphilitic }
		}
		change_diplomacy = 1
		change_stewardship = 1
		change_martial = 1
		change_intrigue = 1
		change_learning = 1
		health = 3
		fertility = 0.2
		set_character_flag = nb_syphilitic
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			trait = leper
			NOT = { has_character_flag = nb_leper }
		}
		add_ten_duel_experience_modifier = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			add_ten_duel_experience_modifier = yes
			add_five_duel_experience_modifier = yes
		}
		change_diplomacy = 2
		health = 3
		fertility = 0.95
		set_character_flag = nb_leper
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			trait = has_tuberculosis
			NOT = { has_character_flag = nb_has_tuberculosis }
		}
		add_ten_duel_experience_modifier = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			add_ten_duel_experience_modifier = yes
			add_five_duel_experience_modifier = yes
		}
		health = 3
		change_diplomacy = 2
		change_intrigue = 2
		change_stewardship = 1
		set_character_flag = nb_has_tuberculosis
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			trait = has_typhoid_fever
			NOT = { has_character_flag = nb_has_typhoid_fever }
		}

	else_if = {

			add_ten_duel_experience_modifier = yes

		health = 8

		change_intrigue = 3
		change_martial = 3
		change_stewardship = 2
		change_learning = 2
		fertility = 0.5
		set_character_flag = nb_has_bubonic_plague
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			trait = has_measles
			NOT = { has_character_flag = nb_has_measles }
		}
		add_ten_duel_experience_modifier = yes
		hidden_tooltip = {
			add_ten_duel_experience_modifier = yes
			add_ten_duel_experience_modifier = yes
		}
		health = 3
