}

# ROOT resists a temptation of the Blood God (khorne.100 - .150): the path is
# closed for good and the strain shows.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
khorne_resist_effect = {
	clr_character_flag = khorne_tempted
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
		add_trait = stressed
	}
}

# ROOT gives in: the mocker at court comes 35 - 105 days later (khorne.150).
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
khorne_give_in_effect = {
	set_character_flag = khorne_tempted
	character_event = { id = khorne.150 days = 35 random = 70 }
}

# ROOT shows the mocker mercy (khorne.150): the path taken is beaten for good.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
khorne_mercy_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = khorne_path_mutant }
		set_character_flag = khorne_beat_mutant
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = khorne_path_proof }
		set_character_flag = khorne_beat_proof
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = khorne_path_scars }
		set_character_flag = khorne_beat_scars
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = khorne_path_hunt }
		set_character_flag = khorne_beat_hunt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = khorne_path_skulls }
		set_character_flag = khorne_beat_skulls
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = khorne_path_war_games }
		set_character_flag = khorne_beat_war_games
	}
	khorne_clear_path_effect = yes
}

# ROOT resists a temptation of the Changer (tzeentch.100 - .150): the path
# is closed for good and the strain shows.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
tzeentch_resist_effect = {
	clr_character_flag = tzeentch_tempted
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { trait = stressed } }
		add_trait = stressed
	}
}

# ROOT gives in: the doubter at court comes 35 - 105 days later
# (tzeentch.150).
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
tzeentch_give_in_effect = {
	set_character_flag = tzeentch_tempted
	character_event = { id = tzeentch.150 days = 35 random = 70 }
}

# ROOT confesses to the doubter (tzeentch.150): the path taken is beaten for
# good.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
tzeentch_mercy_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = tzeentch_path_book }
		set_character_flag = tzeentch_beat_book
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = tzeentch_path_letters }
		set_character_flag = tzeentch_beat_letters
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = tzeentch_path_crown }
		set_character_flag = tzeentch_beat_crown
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = tzeentch_path_flesh }
		set_character_flag = tzeentch_beat_flesh
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = tzeentch_path_augur }
		set_character_flag = tzeentch_beat_augur
	}
	tzeentch_clear_path_effect = yes
}

# The Maggot King's stolen body rots on: schedules the next stage of
# decay (tamurkhan.5), sooner for a weak body, later for a strong one.
# Scope: the host.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_gods_events
tamurkhan_schedule_decay_effect = {
	random_list = {
		5 = {
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = soft }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 5 trait = fragile }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 2 trait = weak }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 2 trait = feeble }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = wounded }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = maimed }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = infirm }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = incapable }
			character_event = { id = tamurkhan.5 days = 360 random = 60 }
		}
		20 = {
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = robust }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = hardy }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = tough }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.3 trait = strong }
			character_event = { id = tamurkhan.5 days = 860 random = 60 }
		}
		20 = {
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = robust }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = hardy }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = tough }
			character_event = { id = tamurkhan.5 days = 740 random = 60 }
		}
		20 = {
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = robust }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = hardy }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = tough }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = strong }
			character_event = { id = tamurkhan.5 days = 960 random = 60 }
		}
		20 = {
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = robust }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = hardy }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = tough }
			mult_modifier = { factor = 5 trait = strong }
			character_event = { id = tamurkhan.5 days = 1460 random = 60 }
		}
	}
}

# Used by WHG_chaos_horde_events

# Clears a settlement's progress flags and counter (end of the chain).
# Scope: the settlement province.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_horde_events
chaoshorde_settlement_reset_effect = {
	clr_province_flag = start_bracket
	clr_province_flag = mid_bracket
	clr_province_flag = top_bracket
	clr_province_flag = development_is_done
	clr_province_flag = absence_of_governor1
	clr_province_flag = absence_of_governor2
	set_variable = { which = chaos_settlement_event_progress value = 0 }
}

# At war and without standing orders, the ruler is asked how to treat
# captured holdings (chaoshorde.38). Scope: the ruler.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_horde_events
chaoshorde_ask_sack_orders_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			war = yes
			NOT = { chaoshorde_sack_orders_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { has_character_flag = chaoshorde_asked_orders }
		}
		set_character_flag = chaoshorde_asked_orders
		character_event = { id = chaoshorde.38 }
	}
}

# Settled (non-nomad) vassals above baron go free when the horde shatters.
# Scope: the dead warlord.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_horde_events
chaoshorde_free_settled_vassals_effect = {
	any_vassal = { limit = { is_nomadic = no higher_tier_than = BARON } set_defacto_liege = THIS }
}

# Creates the warlord of a breakaway province (new_character): a man of the
# heir's people or a beastman, with a culture and god fitting the part of the
# Wastes, and a gift of his god. Scope: the breakaway province.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_horde_events
chaoshorde_create_breakaway_warlord_effect = {
	random_list = {
		10 = {
			create_random_diplomat = {
				random_traits = yes
				culture = event_target:nomadic_heir
				religion = event_target:nomadic_heir
			}
		}
		20 = {
			create_random_soldier = {
				random_traits = yes
				culture = event_target:nomadic_heir
				religion = event_target:nomadic_heir
			}
		}
		10 = {
			create_random_intriguer = {
				random_traits = yes
				culture = event_target:nomadic_heir
				religion = event_target:nomadic_heir
			}
		}
		20 = {
			create_random_steward = {
				random_traits = yes
				culture = event_target:nomadic_heir
				religion = event_target:nomadic_heir
			}
		}
		15 = {
			create_random_priest = {
				random_traits = yes
				culture = event_target:nomadic_heir
				religion = event_target:nomadic_heir
			}
		}
		15 = {
			create_random_intriguer = {
				random_traits = yes
				culture = beastman_main
				religion = event_target:nomadic_heir
			}
		}
		10 = {
			create_random_soldier = {
				random_traits = yes
				culture = beastman_main
				religion = event_target:nomadic_heir
			}
		}
	}
	# The people and gods of each part of the Wastes
	if = {
		limit = { new_character = { culture_group = beastman_group } }
		new_character = {
			random_list = {
				30 = { religion = chaos }
				15 = { religion = khorne }
				10 = { religion = slaanesh }
				12 = { religion = tzeentch }
				15 = { religion = nurgle }
				10 = { }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				region = world_chaos_wastes_eastern_steppes
				region = world_chaos_wastes_zanbaijin
			}
		}
		new_character = {
			random_list = {
				20 = { culture = khazag }
				25 = { culture = yusak }
				20 = { culture = tokmar }
				20 = { culture = kul }
				5 = { culture = dolgan }
				10 = { }
			}
			random_list = {
				20 = { religion = chaos }
				20 = { religion = khorne }
				10 = { religion = slaanesh }
				12 = { religion = tzeentch }
				20 = { religion = nurgle }
				10 = { }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { region = world_chaos_wastes_broken_lands }
		new_character = {
			random_list = {
				20 = { culture = aghol }
				25 = { culture = gharhar }
				20 = { culture = kvellig }
				20 = { culture = tahmak }
				5 = { culture = mung }
				10 = { }
			}
			random_list = {
				10 = { religion = chaos }
				15 = { religion = khorne }
				25 = { religion = slaanesh }
				25 = { religion = tzeentch }
				10 = { religion = nurgle }
				15 = { }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { region = world_chaos_wastes_far_wastes }
		new_character = {
			random_list = {
				35 = { culture = mung }
				20 = { culture = aghol }
				25 = { culture = chi_an }
				5 = { culture = kvellig }
				20 = { }
			}
			random_list = {
				20 = { religion = chaos }
				25 = { religion = khorne }
				20 = { religion = slaanesh }
				25 = { religion = tzeentch }
				10 = { religion = nurgle }
				10 = { }
			}
		}
	}
	# The god's gift
	new_character = {
		if = {
			limit = { religion = khorne }
			random_list = {
				30 = { add_trait = khorne_berserker }
				15 = { add_trait = champion_khorne }
				10 = { add_trait = chaos_knight }
				5 = { add_trait = chosen_khorne }
				40 = { }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { religion = tzeentch }
			random_list = {
				20 = { add_trait = chaos_knight }
				10 = { add_trait = champion_tzeentch }
				10 = {
					add_trait = champion_tzeentch
					add_trait = lore_tzeentch
					random_lore_mage_effect = yes
				}
				15 = { add_trait = lore_tzeentch random_lore_mage_effect = yes }
				5 = { add_trait = chosen_tzeentch }
				40 = { }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { religion = nurgle }
			random_list = {
				20 = { add_trait = chaos_knight }
				10 = { add_trait = champion_nurgle }
				10 = {
					add_trait = chaos_knight
					add_trait = lore_nurgle
					random_lore_mage_effect = yes
				}
				10 = { add_trait = lore_nurgle random_lore_mage_effect = yes }
				5 = { add_trait = chosen_nurgle }
				40 = { }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { religion = slaanesh }
			random_list = {
				20 = { add_trait = chaos_knight }
				15 = { add_trait = champion_slaanesh }
				5 = { add_trait = duelist add_trait = fair }
				10 = { add_trait = lore_slaanesh random_lore_mage_effect = yes }
				5 = { add_trait = chosen_slaanesh }
				40 = { }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { religion = chaos }
			random_list = {
				20 = { add_trait = chaos_knight }
				15 = { add_trait = champion_undivided }
				5 = { add_trait = duelist add_trait = champion_undivided }
				10 = { add_trait = lore_chaos random_lore_mage_effect = yes }
				5 = { add_trait = chaos_chosen }
				45 = { }
			}
		}
	}
}

# The conqueror of Karag Dum is written in the Dammaz Kron, the Great Book of
# Grudges: every Dawi ruler holds a grudge against him (dawi_great_book_effect
# in WHG_dwarf_effects.txt).
# Scope: the conqueror.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_horde_events
chaoshorde_karak_dum_grudge_effect = {
	save_event_target_as = grudge_offender
	dawi_great_book_effect = yes
}

# The four counties of Karag Dum are resettled by the tribes of the four
# gods. Scope: anyone.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_horde_events
chaoshorde_karak_dum_resettle_effect = {
	c_karak_dum = { location = { culture = kul religion = nurgle } }
	c_rodmarin = { location = { culture = baersonling religion = khorne } }
	c_grongol_norn = { location = { culture = hastling religion = tzeentch } }
	c_grung_dwor = { location = { culture = aghol religion = slaanesh } }
}

# The ruler of Jong On, if not a Chaos worshipper, must kneel to the
# conqueror of Karag Dum. Scope: anyone; ROOT is the conqueror.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_horde_events
chaoshorde_karak_dum_jong_on_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { c_jong_on = { holder_scope = { NOT = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group } } } }
		c_jong_on = { holder_scope = { set_defacto_liege = ROOT } }
	}
}

# Four independent champions, one per god, take the counties of Karag Dum,
# each with a warband. Scope: the conqueror.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_horde_events
chaoshorde_karak_dum_warlords_effect = {
	create_random_intriguer = {
		random_traits = yes
		culture = hastling
		religion = tzeentch
		age = 25
		trait = creature_human
		trait = champion_tzeentch
		trait = lore_tzeentch
	}
	new_character = {
		random_lore_mage_effect = yes
		c_grongol_norn = { grant_title = { target = PREV type = invasion } }
		set_defacto_liege = THIS

# Used by: WHG_chaos_horde_events
evaluate_chaos_settlement_progress_effect = {
	event_target:chaos_settlement_province = {
		if = {
			# You're just starting out...
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = chaos_settlement_event_progress
					value = 0
				}
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						which = chaos_settlement_event_progress
						value = 2
					}
				}
			}
			set_province_flag = start_bracket
		}
		else_if = {
			# You're making some progress
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = chaos_settlement_event_progress
					value = 2
				}
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						which = chaos_settlement_event_progress
						value = 4
					}
				}
			}
			clr_province_flag = start_bracket
			set_province_flag = mid_bracket
		}
		else_if = {
			# Get to dueling!
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = chaos_settlement_event_progress
					value = 4
				}
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						which = chaos_settlement_event_progress
						value = 6
					}
				}
			}
			clr_province_flag = start_bracket
			clr_province_flag = mid_bracket
			set_province_flag = top_bracket
			if = {
				limit = {
					#Stream linin' for player runs...
					check_variable = {
						which = chaos_settlement_event_progress
						value = 6
					}					#Nepotism values
				}
				clr_province_flag = start_bracket
				clr_province_flag = mid_bracket
				clr_province_flag = top_bracket
				set_province_flag = development_is_done
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			# Consider yourself victorious, you champ.
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					which = chaos_settlement_event_progress
					value = 6
				}
			}
			clr_province_flag = start_bracket
			clr_province_flag = mid_bracket
			clr_province_flag = top_bracket
			set_province_flag = development_is_done
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_horde_events
add_1_point_to_chaos_settlement_progress_effect = {
	event_target:chaos_settlement_province = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = good_progress_tt
			event_target:chaos_settlement_province = {
				change_variable = {
					which = chaos_settlement_event_progress
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_horde_events
lose_1_point_to_chaos_settlement_progress_effect = {
	event_target:chaos_settlement_province = {
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = good_progress_tt
			event_target:chaos_settlement_province = {
				subtract_variable = {
					which = chaos_settlement_event_progress
					value = 1
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Gains or loses favour with the patron. Scope: a character.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
darkgift_favour_gain_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_khorne_trigger = yes }
		add_offmap_currency = { offmap = khorne_warp value = 500 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_nurgle_trigger = yes }
		add_offmap_currency = { offmap = nurgle_warp value = 500 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
		add_offmap_currency = { offmap = slaanesh_warp value = 500 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_tzeentch_trigger = yes }
		add_offmap_currency = { offmap = tzeentch_warp value = 500 }
	}
	else = { add_offmap_currency = { offmap = undivided_warp value = 500 } }
}

# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
darkgift_favour_loss_effect = {
	if = {

	else_if = {

		add_offmap_currency = { offmap = nurgle_warp value = -500 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
		add_offmap_currency = { offmap = slaanesh_warp value = -500 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_tzeentch_trigger = yes }
		add_offmap_currency = { offmap = tzeentch_warp value = -500 }
	}
	else = { add_offmap_currency = { offmap = undivided_warp value = -500 } }
}

# The patron strips a rank: a Chosen falls back to Champion of the same god
# (Chosen and Champion traits are opposites, so the Champion trait has to be
# given back), a Champion loses the title. Scope: a Champion or Chosen.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
darkgift_cast_down_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { trait = chosen_khorne }
		remove_trait = chosen_khorne
		add_trait = champion_khorne
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { trait = chosen_nurgle }
		remove_trait = chosen_nurgle
		add_trait = champion_nurgle
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { trait = chosen_slaanesh }
		remove_trait = chosen_slaanesh
		add_trait = champion_slaanesh
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { trait = chosen_tzeentch }
		remove_trait = chosen_tzeentch
		add_trait = champion_tzeentch
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { trait = chaos_chosen }
		remove_trait = chaos_chosen
		add_trait = champion_undivided
	}
	else = {
		remove_trait = champion_khorne
		remove_trait = champion_nurgle
		remove_trait = champion_slaanesh
		remove_trait = champion_tzeentch
		remove_trait = champion_undivided
	}
}

# The patron names a marked aspirant Champion. Beastmen become Bestigors, as
# with the champion decisions. Scope: a marked aspirant.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
darkgift_name_champion_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_khorne_trigger = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { culture_group = beastman_group }
			add_trait = bestigor_khorne
			set_graphical_culture = bestigor_khorne
		}
		else = { add_trait = champion_khorne }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_nurgle_trigger = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { culture_group = beastman_group }
			add_trait = bestigor_nurgle
			set_graphical_culture = bestigor_nurgle
		}
		else = { add_trait = champion_nurgle }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { culture_group = beastman_group }
			add_trait = bestigor_slaanesh
			set_graphical_culture = bestigor_slaanesh
		}
		else = { add_trait = champion_slaanesh }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_tzeentch_trigger = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { culture_group = beastman_group }
			add_trait = bestigor_tzeentch
			set_graphical_culture = bestigor_tzeentch
		}
		else = { add_trait = champion_tzeentch }
	}
	else = { add_trait = champion_undivided }
}

# The patron reshapes the character's temper towards its own nature.
# Scope: a character.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
darkgift_temper_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_khorne_trigger = yes }
		random_list = {
			10 = { remove_trait = patient add_trait = wroth }
			10 = { remove_trait = craven add_trait = brave }
			10 = { remove_trait = deceitful add_trait = honest }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_nurgle_trigger = yes }
		random_list = {
			10 = { remove_trait = cruel add_trait = kind }
			10 = { remove_trait = shy add_trait = gregarious }
			10 = { remove_trait = ambitious add_trait = content }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
		random_list = {
			10 = { remove_trait = kind add_trait = cruel }
			10 = { remove_trait = humble add_trait = proud }
			10 = { remove_trait = chaste add_trait = lustful }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_tzeentch_trigger = yes }
		random_list = {
			10 = { remove_trait = trusting add_trait = paranoid }
			10 = { remove_trait = honest add_trait = deceitful }
			10 = { remove_trait = content add_trait = ambitious }
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_list = {
			10 = { remove_trait = cynical add_trait = zealous }
			10 = { remove_trait = humble add_trait = proud }
			10 = { remove_trait = content add_trait = ambitious }
		}
	}
}

# A mutation from the patron's own table. Scope: a character.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
darkgift_mutate_effect = {
	if = { limit = { darkgift_patron_khorne_trigger = yes } mutations_table_khorne = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { darkgift_patron_nurgle_trigger = yes } mutations_table_nurgle = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { darkgift_patron_slaanesh_trigger = yes } mutations_table_slaanesh = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { darkgift_patron_tzeentch_trigger = yes } mutations_table_tzeentch = yes }
	else = { mutations_table = yes }
}

# One step up the physique ladder: weak, fragile, soft, (none), hardy,
# tough, strong. Scope: a character.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
darkgift_physique_effect = {
	if = { limit = { trait = weak } remove_trait = weak add_trait = fragile }
	else_if = { limit = { trait = fragile } remove_trait = fragile add_trait = soft }
	else_if = { limit = { trait = soft } remove_trait = soft }
	else_if = { limit = { trait = hardy } remove_trait = hardy add_trait = tough }
	else_if = { limit = { trait = tough } remove_trait = tough add_trait = strong }
	else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = strong } } add_trait = hardy }
	else = { health = 1 }
}

# The patron's own boon: Khorne, Nurgle and the Four make the body sturdy,
# Slaanesh perfects its beauty, Tzeentch sharpens the mind.
# Scope: a character.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
darkgift_boon_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = groomed } } add_trait = groomed }
		else = { add_trait = fair }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_tzeentch_trigger = yes }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = shrewd } } add_trait = shrewd }
		else = { change_learning = 2 }
	}
	else = {
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = sturdy } } add_trait = sturdy }
		else = { change_martial = 2 }
	}
}

# The patron teaches its own way of life. Scope: a character.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
darkgift_lifestyle_effect = {
	remove_lifestyle_trait_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_khorne_trigger = yes }
		random_list = {
			25 = { add_trait = strategist }
			25 = { add_trait = duelist }
			25 = { add_trait = hunter }
			25 = { add_trait = impaler }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_nurgle_trigger = yes }
		random_list = {
			40 = { add_trait = gardener }
			20 = { add_trait = socializer }
			20 = { add_trait = architect }
			20 = { add_trait = administrator }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_slaanesh_trigger = yes }
		random_list = {
			30 = { add_trait = hedonist }
			20 = { add_trait = socializer }
			20 = { add_trait = duelist }
			30 = { trigger = { is_female = yes } add_trait = seductress }
			30 = { trigger = { is_female = no } add_trait = seducer }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { darkgift_patron_tzeentch_trigger = yes }
		random_list = {
			30 = { add_trait = mystic }
			30 = { add_trait = schemer }
			25 = { add_trait = scholar }
			15 = { add_trait = gamer }
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_list = {
			40 = { add_trait = strategist }
			30 = { add_trait = duelist }
			30 = { add_trait = theologian }
		}
	}
}

# Sponsors the march: zeal, piety, the child's gratitude.
# Scope: a ruler; the child is event_target:childrens_crusade_leader.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
ravenchild_sponsor_effect = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = zealous } } add_trait = zealous }
	tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
	event_target:childrens_crusade_leader = {
		show_scope_change = no
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_believed_in_me who = ROOT years = 15 }
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		event_target:childrens_crusade_leader = {
			save_persistent_event_target = { name = childrens_crusade_sponsor_per scope = ROOT }
		}
	}
}

# Sends warriors to guard the march (heavy troops later).
# Scope: a ruler.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
ravenchild_send_troops_effect = {
	if = { limit = { trait = cruel } random = { chance = 25 remove_trait = cruel } }
	if = {
		limit = { NOR = { trait = kind trait = cruel } }
		random = {
			chance = 25
			add_trait = kind
			hidden_effect = { character_event = { id = traitnote.268 } }
		}
	}
	event_target:childrens_crusade_leader = {
		show_scope_change = no
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_grateful who = ROOT years = 15 }
		hidden_effect = { change_variable = { which = children_crusade_heavy_troops value = 2 } }
	}
	tiered_piety_reward_effect = yes
	capital_scope = {
		add_province_modifier = { name = kid_crusade_pledged_troops duration = 1200 stacking = yes }
	}
}

# Feeds the children and sends them on. Scope: a ruler.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
ravenchild_feed_effect = {
	ravenchild_mercy_effect = yes
	hidden_effect = {
		event_target:childrens_crusade_leader = {
			change_variable = { which = children_crusade_morale value = 2 }
		}
	}
	transfer_scaled_wealth = {
		to = event_target:childrens_crusade_leader
		value = 1
		min = 50
		max = 100
	}
}

# Guards herd the children on; morale drops. Scope: a ruler.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
ravenchild_guards_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		event_target:childrens_crusade_leader = {
			change_variable = { which = children_crusade_morale value = -1 }
		}
	}
}

# The march disbands: followers vanish, the band's title is destroyed.
# Scope: the child.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
ravenchild_disband_effect = {
	any_courtier = {
		limit = { is_ruler = no is_landed = no host = { character = ROOT } }
		if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
		else = { death = { death_reason = death_missing } }
	}
	primary_title = { activate_title = { title = THIS status = no } destroy_landed_title = THIS }
}

# The ruler sells the child and the young followers to the slavers.
# Scope: a ruler.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
ravenchild_sell_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		event_target:childrens_crusade_leader = {
			any_courtier = {
				limit = { is_ruler = no is_adult = no }
				if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
				else = { death = { death_reason = death_slavery } }
			}
			primary_title = {
				activate_title = { title = THIS status = no }
				destroy_landed_title = THIS
			}
			if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
			else = { death = { death_reason = death_slavery } }
		}
	}
}

# Moves the march to its next stop and tells the players of the Dark Gods;
# at Zanbaijin the war begins (ravenchild.21).
# Scope: the child (ravenchild.9).
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
ravenchild_move_effect = {
	if = { # Kislev fell to Chaos, or a Black Crusade is under way: the march scatters
		limit = {
			OR = {
				k_kislev = { has_holder = yes holder_scope = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group } }
				c_kislev = { holder_scope = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group } }
				c_kislev = { holder_scope = { is_crusade_target = yes } }
				religion_head = { is_preparing_crusade = yes }
				religion_head = {
					OR = { any_war = { using_cb = crusade } any_war = { using_cb = new_crusade } }
				}
			}
		}
		any_player = {
			limit = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group NOT = { character = ROOT } }
			narrative_event = { id = ravenchild.20 days = 30 }
		}
		primary_title = {
			activate_title = { title = THIS status = no }
			destroy_landed_title = THIS
		}
		clr_character_flag = flag_childrens_crusade_leader
		add_trait = lunatic_2
		set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 10 }
		piety = -150
		remove_trait = disinherited
		set_variable = { which = children_crusade_forces value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = children_crusade_morale value = 0 }
		set_variable = { which = children_crusade_heavy_troops value = 0 }
		any_courtier = { remove_trait = disinherited clr_character_flag = no_court_invites }
	}
	else_if = { # First stop: the Far Wastes
		limit = { has_character_flag = flag_children_at_beginning }
		primary_title = { set_preferred_capital = c_the_ragged_maze } # The Inevitable City
		change_variable = { which = children_crusade_forces value = 1 }
		change_variable = { which = children_crusade_morale value = -1 }
		clr_character_flag = flag_children_at_beginning
		set_character_flag = flag_children_in_far_wastes
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { persistent_event_target:childrens_crusade_sponsor_per = { is_alive = yes } }
			}
			change_variable = { which = children_crusade_morale value = -2 }
		}
		any_player = {
			limit = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group NOT = { character = ROOT } }
			narrative_event = { id = ravenchild.16 days = 30 }
		}
		narrative_event = { id = ravenchild.9 days = 50 random = 100 }
	}
	else_if = { # Then the Broken Lands
		limit = { has_character_flag = flag_children_in_far_wastes }
		primary_title = { set_preferred_capital = c_eiigin }
		change_variable = { which = children_crusade_morale value = -1 }
		clr_character_flag = flag_children_in_far_wastes
		set_character_flag = flag_children_in_broken_lands
		any_player = {
			limit = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group NOT = { character = ROOT } }
			narrative_event = { id = ravenchild.17 days = 30 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { persistent_event_target:childrens_crusade_sponsor_per = { is_alive = yes } }
			}
			random = {
				chance = 25
				change_variable = { which = children_crusade_forces value = -1 }
			}
		}
		narrative_event = { id = ravenchild.9 days = 50 random = 100 }
	}
	else_if = { # Then the Eastern Steppes
		limit = { has_character_flag = flag_children_in_broken_lands }
		primary_title = { set_preferred_capital = c_kharakit }
		clr_character_flag = flag_children_in_broken_lands
		set_character_flag = flag_children_in_eastern_steppes
		change_variable = { which = children_crusade_forces value = 1 }
		any_player = {
			limit = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group NOT = { character = ROOT } }
			narrative_event = { id = ravenchild.18 days = 30 }
		}
		narrative_event = { id = ravenchild.9 days = 50 random = 100 }
	}
	else_if = { # Zanbaijin at last: the war begins
		limit = { has_character_flag = flag_children_in_eastern_steppes }
		primary_title = { set_preferred_capital = c_zanbaijin } # Where the armies gather
		clr_character_flag = flag_children_in_eastern_steppes
		set_character_flag = flag_children_in_zanbaijin
		narrative_event = { id = ravenchild.21 days = 25 }
		any_player = {
			limit = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group NOT = { character = ROOT } }
			narrative_event = { id = ravenchild.19 days = 30 }
		}
	}
	else = { # Fallback: straight to Zanbaijin
		primary_title = { set_preferred_capital = c_zanbaijin }
		clr_character_flag = flag_children_in_far_wastes
		clr_character_flag = flag_children_in_broken_lands
		clr_character_flag = flag_children_in_eastern_steppes
		set_character_flag = flag_children_in_zanbaijin
		change_variable = { which = children_crusade_forces value = 1 }
		change_variable = { which = children_crusade_morale value = -1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { persistent_event_target:childrens_crusade_sponsor_per = { is_alive = yes } }
			}
			change_variable = { which = children_crusade_morale value = -2 }
		}
		narrative_event = { id = ravenchild.21 days = 25 }
		any_player = {
			limit = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group NOT = { character = ROOT } }
			narrative_event = { id = ravenchild.19 days = 30 }
		}
	}
}

# Spawns light troops until children_crusade_forces is spent. Scope: the child.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
ravenchild_light_troops_effect = {
	change_variable = { which = children_crusade_forces value = -1 }
	location = {
		ROOT = {
			spawn_unit = {
				province = PREV
				home = PREV
				owner = ROOT
				attrition = 0.5
				reinforces = no
				is_looter = no
				can_toggle_looting = no
				cannot_inherit = yes
				merge = yes
				disband_on_peace = yes
				earmark = childrens_crusade_infantry_earmark
				troops = { light_infantry = { 3000 3000 } archers = { 100 100 } }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = children_crusade_forces value = 1 } }
		character_event = { id = ravenchild.22 }
	}
}

		ROOT = {
			spawn_unit = {
				province = PREV
				home = PREV
				owner = ROOT
				attrition = 0.5
				reinforces = no
				is_looter = no
				can_toggle_looting = no
				cannot_inherit = yes
				merge = yes
				troops = { heavy_infantry = { 500 500 } light_cavalry = { 150 150 } }
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { which = children_crusade_heavy_troops value = 1 } }
		character_event = { id = ravenchild.23 }
	}
}

# One daemon host for a new Everchosen (undivided.40): 20,700 men at the

	location = {
		ROOT = {
			spawn_unit = {
				province = PREV
				troops = {
					horse_archers = { 2700 2700 }
					light_cavalry = { 2500 2500 }
					light_infantry = { 5000 5000 }
					heavy_infantry = { 5000 5000 }
					knights = { 500 500 }
					archers = { 5000 5000 }
				}
				earmark = everchosen_troops
			}
		}
	}
}

# Marks a trial champion for a duel to the death with the seeker
# (event_target:combatant_1) and has it vanish afterwards (undivided.37).
# Scope: the champion. ROOT = the seeker.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events, WHG_chaos_effects
undivided_trial_champion_setup_effect = {
	set_character_flag = duel
	set_character_flag = flag_duel_no_draws
	set_character_flag = deadly_duel@event_target:combatant_1
	set_character_flag = fought_duel_against@ROOT
	character_event = { id = undivided.37 days = 21 }
}

}

# Gives each artifact of the Everchosen the character does not hold yet, so
# every path (AI, default rule or the extended quest) ends with all five.
# Scope: the Everchosen.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
undivided_give_missing_artifacts_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_artifact = armor_of_morkar } }
		add_artifact = armor_of_morkar
		new_artifact = { set_creation_date = 1.1.1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_artifact = eye_of_sheerian } }
		add_artifact = eye_of_sheerian
		new_artifact = { set_creation_date = 1.1.1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_artifact = slayer_of_kings } }
		add_artifact = slayer_of_kings
		new_artifact = { set_creation_date = 1.1.1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_artifact = steed_of_the_apocalypse_mount } }
		add_artifact = steed_of_the_apocalypse_mount
		new_artifact = { set_creation_date = 1.1.1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_artifact = crown_of_domination } }
		add_artifact = crown_of_domination
		new_artifact = { set_creation_date = 1.1.1 }
	}
}

# Ends the quest: flags, stage, the artifacts won so far, and the claim on
# the Mark, so another seeker can try.
# Scope: the seeker.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
undivided_quest_reset_effect = {
	undivided_quest_clear_stage_effect = yes
	clr_character_flag = potential_everchosen
	clr_character_flag = everchosen_marked
	clr_character_flag = everchosen_has_dorghar
	clr_character_flag = everchosen_has_slayer
	clr_character_flag = everchosen_on_dorghar_road
	clr_global_flag = everchosen_quest_claimed
	undivided_destroy_artifacts_effect = yes
	if = { limit = { trait = on_pilgrimage } remove_trait = on_pilgrimage }
}

# The Everchosen is crowned: the Winds of Chaos blow at their strongest.
# Sets the doom counter to its maximum and the very high flag at once
# (doom.1 would set it on its next pass anyway).
# Scope: any.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
undivided_doom_to_max_effect = {
	custom_tooltip = { text = undivided_doom_to_max_tt }
	hidden_effect = {
		1 = { set_variable = { which = "global_doom_counter" value = 1500 } }
		set_global_flag = doom_very_high
		clr_global_flag = doom_very_low
		clr_global_flag = doom_low
		clr_global_flag = doom_medium
		clr_global_flag = doom_high
	}
}

# The host of the necropolis (undivided.55): the shade of Morkar takes a
# temporary title and a host of the dead matched to the seeker's army, on the
# seeker's island, hostile to the seeker for a year so the armies fight at
# once. The host is a little weaker at very high doom, a little stronger at
# high. The battle is settled by undivided.74 (won) and undivided.75 (lost);
# undivided.76 clears the dead after a year.
# Scope: ROOT = the seeker.
# Used by: WHG_chaos_undivided_events
undivided_necropolis_host_effect = {
	create_random_soldier = {
		name = "Morkar"
		random_traits = no
		dynasty = none
		religion = chaos
		culture = kul
		female = no
		age = 45
		attributes = { martial = 18 diplomacy = 4 stewardship = 2 intrigue = 6 learning = 5 }
		trait = creature_human
		trait = wroth
		trait = brave
	}
	new_character = {
		save_event_target_as = necropolis_shade
		give_nickname = nick_the_uniter
		set_character_flag = necropolis_guardian
		set_looter_hostility_days = { who = ROOT days = 365 }
	}
	create_title = {
		tier = DUKE
		name = "UNDIVIDED_NECROPOLIS_HOST"
		culture = event_target:necropolis_shade
		ruler = "UNDIVIDED_NECROPOLIS_SHADE"
		ruler_female = "UNDIVIDED_NECROPOLIS_SHADE"
		landless = yes
		temporary = yes
		adventurer = yes
		holder = event_target:necropolis_shade
	}
	location = {
		ROOT = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_doom_very_high_trigger = yes }
				spawn_unit = {
					owner = event_target:necropolis_shade
					leader = event_target:necropolis_shade
					province = PREV
					match_character = ROOT
					match_mult = 0.7
					troops = {
						light_infantry = { 800 800 }
						heavy_infantry = { 600 600 }
						archers = { 300 300 }
					}
					earmark = necropolis_dead
					attrition = 0
					maintenance_multiplier = 0
					disband_on_peace = no
				}
			}
			else = {
				spawn_unit = {
					owner = event_target:necropolis_shade
					leader = event_target:necropolis_shade
					province = PREV
					match_character = ROOT
					match_mult = 1.0
					troops = {
						light_infantry = { 800 800 }
						heavy_infantry = { 600 600 }
						archers = { 300 300 }
					}
					earmark = necropolis_dead
					attrition = 0
					maintenance_multiplier = 0
					disband_on_peace = no
				}
			}
		}
	}
	event_target:necropolis_shade = { character_event = { id = undivided.76 days = 365 } }
}

# A prisoner has been fed to a daemon. The summoner's Old World and elven
# vassals and the victim's kin hold it against the summoner.
# Scope: the victim (ROOT), FROM the summoner.
# Used by: WHG_daemon_events
daemon_victim_outrage_effect = {
	FROM = {
		any_vassal = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					religion = cadai
					religion = asrai_cadai
					religion_group = old_world_group
				}
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_evil_sorcerer who = FROM }
		}
	}
	any_dynasty_member = {
		limit = { NOT = { character = FROM } }
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_destroyed_family who = FROM }
	}
}

# A summoning into a prisoner failed: a Chaos cult quest on that prisoner
# fails with it.
# Scope: the summoner (ROOT), FROM the prisoner.
# Used by: WHG_daemon_events
daemon_summon_failed_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_quest = quest_chaos_cult_demonic_possession
			quest_target = { character = FROM }
		}
		clr_quest = { id = quest_chaos_cult_demonic_possession failure = yes }
	}
}

# A daemon now wears the prisoner's body and serves the summoner. Completes a
# Chaos cult possession quest on that prisoner.
# Scope: the summoner (ROOT), FROM the new daemon.
# Used by: WHG_daemon_events
daemon_summon_bound_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_quest = quest_chaos_cult_demonic_possession
			quest_target = { character = FROM }
		}
		clr_quest = quest_chaos_cult_demonic_possession
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_female = yes } }
			sound_effect = devil_worshippers_demonic_possession_female
		}
		else = {
			sound_effect = devil_worshippers_demonic_possession_male
		}
		add_society_currency_medium_effect = yes
	}
	add_friend = FROM
	set_character_flag = daemon_summoner
	prestige = 500
	FROM = { prisoner = no }
}

# The daemon possessing a character grows stronger: three times in five the
# possession counter rises by 5 (small), 10 (medium) or 15 (big). Otherwise the
# host fights back and it falls by 3 to 5. Sharp minds, wizards and the learned
# resist best; dull minds and the wild-gifted worst.
# Scope: the possessed character.
# Used by: WHG_daemon_events
daemon_possession_small_effect = {
	random_list = {
		60 = {
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = imbecile }
			modifier = { factor = 1.95 trait = idiot }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = slow }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = dumb }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = dim }
			modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = bright }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = smart }
			modifier = { factor = 0.65 trait = quick }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = brilliant }
			modifier = { factor = 0.45 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = magic_potential_4 }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_4 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.95 has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.85 has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.65 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.95 learning = 15 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.85 learning = 20 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 learning = 25 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.65 learning = 30 }
			change_variable = { which = "possession_counter" value = 5 }
		}
		20 = { change_variable = { which = "possession_counter" value = -3 } }
		10 = { change_variable = { which = "possession_counter" value = -4 } }
		10 = {
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 learning = 30 }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = genius }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
			change_variable = { which = "possession_counter" value = -5 }
		}
	}
}

	clr_character_flag = possessed_by_nurglite
	clr_character_flag = possessed_by_slaaneshi
	clr_character_flag = possessed_by_tzeentchian

		add_doom_progress_massive_effect = yes
	}
	else = {
		add_doom_progress_major_effect = yes
	}
}

		}

	}
}

# Used by WHG_slaanesh_events

# Finds the Cult of Pleasure's grand master and saves them as
# event_target:grand_master.
# Scope: any.
# Used by: WHG_slaanesh_events
pleasure_find_grand_master_effect = {
	cult_of_pleasure = {
		leader = { save_event_target_as = grand_master }
	}
}

# Sets the flags GetDWReasonToJoin reads, so the recruit asks the cult for
# help with their own trouble: a sick lover, spouse or child, debts, their
# own illness, a war or a rival.
# Scope: the recruit.
# Used by: WHG_slaanesh_events
pleasure_reason_to_join_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { any_lover = { has_disease_trigger = yes } }
		set_character_flag = has_ill_lover_loc
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_spouse = { has_disease_trigger = yes } }
		set_character_flag = has_ill_spouse_loc
	}
	if = {
		limit = { any_child = { has_disease_trigger = yes } }
		set_character_flag = has_ill_child_loc
	}
	if = { limit = { NOT = { wealth = 0 } } set_character_flag = has_money_troubles_loc }
	if = { limit = { has_disease_trigger = yes } set_character_flag = has_some_illness_loc }
	if = { limit = { war = yes } set_character_flag = has_need_of_winning_battles_loc }
	if = { limit = { any_rival = { always = yes } } set_character_flag = has_a_rival_loc }
}

# No member of the right sex is beautiful enough to meet the recruit
# (pleasure.18), so the cult sends a new seducer, who joins the cult with
# Atharti as their secret faith. Saves them as
# event_target:cult_of_pleasure_inductor.
# Scope: the new character. ROOT is the recruit.
# Used by: WHG_slaanesh_events
pleasure_new_inductor_effect = {
	character_event = { id = aiclean.31 } # Set racial trait
	remove_trait = ugly
	remove_trait = hunchback
	remove_trait = dwarf
	remove_trait = chaste
	add_trait = lustful
	add_trait = fair
	join_society = cult_of_pleasure
	set_secret_religion = atharti
	save_event_target_as = cult_of_pleasure_inductor
}

# The recruit joins the cult and takes Atharti as their secret faith, then the
# recruitment ends.
# Scope: the recruit.
# Used by: WHG_slaanesh_events
pleasure_join_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_character_flag = being_recruited_by_the_cult_of_pleasure }
		join_society = cult_of_pleasure
		set_secret_religion = atharti
	}
	hidden_effect = { clr_character_flag = being_recruited_by_the_cult_of_pleasure }
}

# A cultist (event_target:pleasure_knife) tries to kill the grand master's
# target (event_target:pleasure_knife_target). About 40% to succeed, better
# with a cunning killer, worse against a paranoid target, never against an
# invincible one. A kill clears the mark for death; a miss may expose the
# killer to the target. The grand master gets a letter either way.
# Scope: the grand master.
# Used by: WHG_slaanesh_events
pleasure_knife_strike_effect = {
	random_list = {
		40 = {
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 event_target:pleasure_knife = { intrigue >= 12 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 event_target:pleasure_knife = { intrigue >= 18 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 event_target:pleasure_knife = { trait = elusive_shadow } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 event_target:pleasure_knife_target = { trait = paranoid } }
			modifier = { factor = 0 event_target:pleasure_knife_target = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } }
			event_target:pleasure_knife_target = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_character_flag = cop_deathmark }
					clr_character_flag = cop_deathmark
					ROOT = { clr_character_flag = cop_marked_someone_for_death }
				}
				death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown killer = event_target:pleasure_knife }
			}
			event_target:pleasure_knife = { add_society_currency_minor_effect = yes }
			letter_event = { id = pleasure.61 days = 7 }
		}
		60 = {
			random = {
				chance = 30

				event_target:pleasure_knife_target = {
					opinion = { modifier = opinion_murder_attempt who = event_target:pleasure_knife }
				}
				set_character_flag = pleasure_knife_exposed
			}
			letter_event = { id = pleasure.62 days = 7 }
		}
	}
}

# The character stops hiding: they leave the secret cult and take up the open
# Slaaneshi faith of their people (the Cult of Pleasure for Asur and Asrai,
# Atharti for Druchii, Slaanesh for everyone else). A ruler of ten years or
# more has already turned their capital.
# Used by: WHG_slaanesh_events
slaanesh_open_worship_effect = {
	leave_society = yes
	if = { limit = { religion_group = cadai_group } religion = cult_of_pleasure }
	else_if = { limit = { religion_group = cytharai_group } religion = atharti }
	else = { religion = slaanesh }
	if = { limit = { is_ruler = yes ruled_years = 10 } capital_scope = { religion = ROOT } }
}

# The polymorph spell (slaanesh.400). ROOT is the caster, FROM the prisoner.
# The caster's slaanesh_polymorph_* flag says which spell was cast: success,
# the prisoner resists, or the prisoner turns the spell back.
# Used by: WHG_slaanesh_events
slaanesh_polymorph_roll_effect = {
	random_list = {
		50 = {
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROM = { trait = avatar_isha } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROM = { trait = avatar_kurnous } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 FROM = { trait = everqueen } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 FROM = { trait = malekith } }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = hedge_wizard }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 FROM = { trait = hedge_wizard } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_monster }
				FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.401 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_toad }
				FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.407 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_pig }
				FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.412 } }
			}
			else = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.417 } } }
		}
		40 = {
			FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.403 } }
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROM = {
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.95 has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.95 has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_3 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.85 has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_4 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = magic_potential_3 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = magic_potential_4 }
		}
		10 = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_monster }
				FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.405 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_toad }
				FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.410 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_pig }
				FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.415 } }
			}
			else = { FROM = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.419 } } }

			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				FROM = {
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
					NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
				}
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 0.25
				FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
			}
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.75 FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 } }
			modifier = { factor = 2 FROM = { has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.95 has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.95 has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_3 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.85 has_character_flag = magic_potential_flag_4 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = magic_potential_3 }
			modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = magic_potential_4 }
		}
	}

	clr_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_monster
	clr_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_toad
	clr_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_pig
	clr_character_flag = slaanesh_polymorph_swap
}

# A prisoner was polymorphed. ROOT is the victim, FROM the caster. The caster's
# Asur vassals call it barbaric and the victim's family never forgets.
# Used by: WHG_slaanesh_events
slaanesh_polymorph_outrage_effect = {
	hidden_effect = {
		FROM = {
			any_vassal = {
				limit = { religion = cadai }
				opinion = { modifier = opinion_barbaric who = FROM }
			}
		}
		any_dynasty_member = {
			limit = { religion_group = cytharai_group }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_polymorphed_family_druchii who = FROM }
		}
		any_dynasty_member = {
			limit = { NOT = { religion_group = cytharai_group } }
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_polymorphed_family who = FROM }
		}
	}
}

# The caster's court and vassals saw what happened to the prisoner. All but the
# brave and the clever are scared into line.
# Used by: WHG_slaanesh_events
slaanesh_scare_court_effect = {
	any_vassal = {
		limit = { NOR = { trait = brave trait = quick trait = brilliant trait = genius } }
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_scared_into_submission who = ROOT }
	}
	any_courtier = {
		limit = { NOR = { trait = brave trait = quick trait = brilliant trait = genius } }
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_scared_into_submission who = ROOT }
	}
}

# Slaanesh changes the character's sex. Portraits switch to the swapped

# and 453, which also tell the spouse).

	else_if = { limit = { graphical_culture = tileangfx } set_graphical_culture = tileanrev }
	else_if = { limit = { graphical_culture = estaliangfx } set_graphical_culture = estalianrev }

		limit = { graphical_culture = nagarythiangfx }
		set_graphical_culture = nagarythianrev
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { graphical_culture = caledoriangfx }
		set_graphical_culture = caledorianrev
	}
	add_trait = transgender
	set_character_flag = slaanesh_gender_swapped
	if = { limit = { trait = creature_beastman is_female = yes } add_trait = horns_small }
	else_if = {
		limit = { trait = creature_beastman }
		remove_trait = horns_small

		remove_trait = horns_big
		add_trait = horns_none

		character_event = { id = slaanesh.451 days = 190 random = 120 }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = homosexual }
			character_event = { id = slaanesh.452 days = 90 random = 90 }
		}
		else = { character_event = { id = slaanesh.453 days = 90 random = 90 } }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Used by WHG_warpstone_events
###############################################################################

# Opens a wyrdstone mine in the province's capital holding, of the kind
# that fits it (castle, city, temple, tribe or nomad).
# Scope: a province.
# Used by: WHG_warpstone_events
warpstone_open_mine_effect = {
	capital_holding = {
		add_building = ct_blackstone_mine_1
		add_building = ca_blackstone_mine_1
		add_building = tp_blackstone_mine_1
		add_building = tb_blackstone_mine_1
		add_building = no_blackstone_mine_1
	}
}

# The witch hunters, priests or runesmiths purge a vein or meteor: the stone
# is gone and no mine can be dug. Old World faithful also call a witch
# hunter of their faith from the realm, if one is free (whunter.2).
# Scope: the province. ROOT: the province.
# Used by: WHG_warpstone_events
warpstone_purge_effect = {
	remove_province_modifier = warpstone_vein
	remove_province_modifier = warpstone_meteor
	set_variable = { which = blackstone_mining_progress value = 0 }
	owner = {
		piety = 50
		if = {
			limit = { religion_group = old_world_group }
			save_event_target_as = whunter_client # the ruler whunter.3 goes to
			random_realm_character = {
				limit = {
					religion = PREV
					is_witch_hunter_trigger = yes
					NOT = { trait = on_witch_hunt }
				}
				character_event = { id = whunter.2 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# A wyrdstone deposit is found: marks the world as having one, so the yearly
# mine check (warpstone.25) only runs once there is anything to mine.
# Scope: the province.
# Used by: WHG_warpstone_events
warpstone_deposit_found_effect = {
	set_global_flag = warpstone_deposits
	set_variable = { which = blackstone_mining_progress value = 0 }
}

# The Mordheim agent is lost in the ruins: slain, unless they cannot die.
# The patron may fund another expedition.
# Scope: the agent. The caller clears the patron's maintaining_agents flag.
# Used by: WHG_warpstone_events
warpstone_agent_lost_effect = {
	clr_character_flag = intrigue_agent_mordheim
	clr_character_flag = mercenary_agent_mordheim
	remove_trait = travelling
	if = {
		limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes }
		antideath_effect = yes
	}
	else = {
		death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown }
	}
}

# The Mordheim agent comes home.
# Scope: the agent.
# Used by: WHG_warpstone_events
warpstone_agent_recall_effect = {
	clr_character_flag = intrigue_agent_mordheim
	clr_character_flag = mercenary_agent_mordheim
	clr_character_flag = mark_for_fail
	clr_character_flag = mark_for_fail2
	remove_trait = travelling
}

# A faithful of the Old World or of Ormazd who deals in the shadow market
# may be talked about (warpstone.33, 15 in 100, a month later).
# Scope: the dealer.
# Used by: WHG_warpstone_events
warpstone_market_whispers_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				religion_group = old_world_group
				religion_group = ormazd_gods_group
			}
		}
		random = {
			chance = 15
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 intrigue = 16 }
			character_event = { id = warpstone.33 days = 30 }
		}
	}
}

# Other helpers used by one or two files
###############################################################################

# Pays for a Mordheim expedition (see warpstone_expedition_can_pay_trigger).
# Scope: the patron.
# Used by: wh_blackstone_decisions
warpstone_expedition_pay_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { warpstone_expedition_pays_piety_trigger = yes }
		piety = -500
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { warpstone_expedition_pays_prestige_trigger = yes }
		prestige = -750
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { trait = creature_skaven }
		wealth = -150
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ai = yes }
		wealth = -200
	}
	else = {
		wealth = -300
	}
}

		}
		10 = {

				factor = 0
				trait = fever
			}
			character_event = { id = symptom.2 }
		}
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				trait = cramps
			}
			character_event = { id = symptom.7 }
		}
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				trait = vomiting
			}
			character_event = { id = symptom.4 }
		}
	}
}

# Marks that this warp realm's tide just turned, so it holds for a few years
# before it can turn again.
# Scope: a warp realm (offmap power).
# Used by: WHG_incursion_events
warp_tide_turned_effect = {
	clr_offmap_flag = warp_tide_turned
	set_offmap_flag = warp_tide_turned
}

# An AI Norscan picks a patron god, weighted by tribe, temper, the faith of
# the capital and the realm's pantheon law.
# Scope: the Norscan (no patron yet).
# Used by: WHG_norscan_gods_events, wh_norscan_decisions
norscan_pick_patron_effect = {
	random_list = {
		20 = { # Kjorn
			modifier = { factor = 0 norscan_dark_patron_allowed_trigger = no }
			modifier = { factor = 0 norscan_old_gods_culture_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 10 capital_scope = { religion = khorne } }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_kharneth
		}
		20 = { # Nierg
			modifier = { factor = 0 norscan_dark_patron_allowed_trigger = no }
			modifier = { factor = 0 norscan_old_gods_culture_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = wroth }
			modifier = { factor = 10 capital_scope = { religion = nurgle } }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_nurgleth
		}
		20 = { # Loesh
			modifier = { factor = 0 norscan_dark_patron_allowed_trigger = no }
			modifier = { factor = 1.25 norscan_old_gods_culture_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = lustful }
			modifier = { factor = 0.1 NOT = { trait = lustful } }
			modifier = { factor = 10 capital_scope = { religion = slaanesh } }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_shornaal
		}
		15 = { # Tchar
			modifier = { factor = 0 norscan_dark_patron_allowed_trigger = no }
			modifier = { factor = 0.66 norscan_old_gods_culture_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 15 capital_scope = { religion = tzeentch } }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_tchar
		}
		5 = { # Olric
			modifier = { factor = 0 norscan_old_patron_allowed_trigger = no }
			modifier = { factor = 0 norscan_old_gods_culture_trigger = no NOT = { culture = varg } }
			modifier = { factor = 5 norscan_old_gods_culture_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = brave }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = proud }
			modifier = { factor = 1.6 culture = skaeling }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_ulric
		}
		5 = { # Orsen
			modifier = { factor = 0 culture = varg }
			modifier = { factor = 0 norscan_old_patron_allowed_trigger = no }
			modifier = { factor = 5 norscan_old_gods_culture_trigger = yes }
			modifier = { factor = 1.6 norscan_old_gods_culture_trigger = yes NOT = { culture = skaeling } }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = slothful }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = temperate }
			modifier = { factor = 2.6 tier = king }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_ursun
		}
	}
}

# Close kin at court who follow no patron take up the same god.
# Scope: the Norscan who just chose a patron.
# Used by: WHG_norscan_gods_events, wh_norscan_decisions
norscan_share_patron_effect = {
	save_event_target_as = norscan_patron_chooser
	any_close_relative = {
		limit = {
			host = { character = event_target:norscan_patron_chooser }
			has_norscan_patron_trigger = no
			OR = {
				religion = norscan_gods
				religion = norscan_gods_reformed
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:norscan_patron_chooser = { trait = norscan_patron_kharneth } }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_kharneth
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:norscan_patron_chooser = { trait = norscan_patron_shornaal } }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_shornaal
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:norscan_patron_chooser = { trait = norscan_patron_nurgleth } }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_nurgleth
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:norscan_patron_chooser = { trait = norscan_patron_tchar } }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_tchar
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:norscan_patron_chooser = { trait = norscan_patron_ulric } }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_ulric
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:norscan_patron_chooser = { trait = norscan_patron_ursun } }
			add_trait = norscan_patron_ursun
		}
	}
}

# A Norscan gives the old gods up for the Dark Gods.
# Scope: the convert. Pays from the warband's favour when given the chance.
# Used by: WHG_norscan_gods_events
norscan_dark_pact_cost_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_member_of_any_warband_trigger = yes
			society_currency >= 1000
		}
		change_society_currency = -1000
		piety = -250
	}
	else = {
		piety = -1000
	}
}

# A province cleared of Skaven is resettled (skaven.71). Tomb Kings bring
# the living of Nehekhara; the Lost of the jade vampires bring their own;
# Blood Dragons and the witch hunter peoples, who do not settle, bring the
# people of the region (or, beyond the known lands, the Strigany who serve
# the vampires and the Reiklanders who follow the hunters). Anyone else
# brings their own people and faith.
# Scope: the province. ROOT: the lord.
# Used by: WHG_skaven_events
skaven_resettle_province_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { trait = creature_nehekharan } }
		culture = nehekka
		religion = atruhayid
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { culture = living_jade } }
		culture = lizardman_lost
		religion = vampiric
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = {
				OR = {
					culture = blooddragons_culture
					is_hunter_culture_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		religion = ROOT
		if = {
			limit = { region = world_old_world_empire }
			culture = reiklander
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_old_world_border_princes }
			culture = neueslander
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_old_world_kislev }
			culture = kislevite
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_troll_country }
			culture = ropsmann
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_old_world_bretonnia }
			culture = nortagnese
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_old_world_estalia }
			culture = astur
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_old_world_tilea }
			culture = reman
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_badlands_strygos }
			culture = strigany
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_badlands_magnata }
			culture = myken
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_greater_araby_araby }
			culture = ramaccan
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_greater_araby_southlands }
			culture = wasai
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_nehekhara }
			culture = nehekka
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_norsca }
			culture = aesling
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { region = world_chaos_wastes }
			culture = kul
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { ROOT = { culture = blooddragons_culture } }
			culture = strigany
		}
		else = {
			culture = reiklander
		}
	}
	else = {
		culture = ROOT
		religion = ROOT
	}
}

# A child shows the signs of the wolf (wolf.2, wolf.10, wolf.20).
# Scope: the child.
# Used by: WHG_wolf_events
wolf_child_gain_effect = {
	add_character_modifier = {
		name = wolf_child
		duration = -1
	}
	hidden_tooltip = { set_character_flag = wolf_blood_first_generation }
}

# The wolf's blood for life; wolf_blood_carrier is the pre-trigger of the
# yearly wolf.200 tombola (wolf.3, wolf.40).
# Scope: the carrier.
# Used by: WHG_wolf_events
wolf_blood_gain_effect = {
	remove_character_modifier = wolf_child
	add_character_modifier = {
		name = wolf_blood
		duration = -1
	}
	hidden_tooltip = { set_character_flag = wolf_blood_carrier }
}

# Picks what the werewolf does under the moon and marks it with a
# wolf_night_* flag for wolf.110 and the morning after (wolf.111).
# Scope: the werewolf.
# Used by: WHG_wolf_events
wolf_moon_night_pick_effect = {
	random_list = {
		10 = { set_character_flag = wolf_night_howl }
		10 = { set_character_flag = wolf_night_swim }
		10 = { set_character_flag = wolf_night_cow }
		10 = { set_character_flag = wolf_night_pig }
		10 = { set_character_flag = wolf_night_dog }
		10 = { set_character_flag = wolf_night_peasant }
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { any_courtier = { at_location = ROOT wolf_prey_trigger = yes } }
			}
			set_character_flag = wolf_night_courtier
		}
		10 = {
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = {
					location = {
						any_neighbor_province = {
							owner = {
								any_courtier = {
									at_location = PREVPREV
									wolf_prey_trigger = yes
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_character_flag = wolf_night_neighbour
		}
		2 = {
			# Rare: the wolf does not know its own.
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				NOT = { any_courtier = { at_location = ROOT wolf_family_prey_trigger = yes } }
			}
			set_character_flag = wolf_night_family
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Not used by any live script yet: kept for content to come
###############################################################################

# Example:
# In a script file:
# Naming convention:  xxxx_effect
join_prev_norscan_society_society = {
	#join the society that own the button you pressed to join...
	if = {
		#if your mod have several societies, you can copy this "if" and make use it for the second society, this way this scripted effect will work for both society.
		limit = {
			PREV = {
				society_member_of = norscan_society
			}
		}
		join_society = norscan_society
	}
	# }
}

join_from_norscan_society_society = {
	#join the society of someone
	FROM = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				society_member_of = norscan_society
			}
			ROOT = {
				join_society = norscan_society
			}
		}
	}
}
